<current_focus>
**Chapter 4: Visitor Control & NFC Access System**
Implementing security/vigilance portal with tablet-friendly UI and NFC card management. Key requirement: NFC cards bind to Properties (1 Card = 1 Unit), not Owners, allowing owners with multiple units to have separate cards per condominium.
</current_focus>

<environment>
- **Stack**: Node.js + Express, SQL Server 2022, EJS + Tailwind CSS, JWT Auth
- **DB Migrations**: 10 migrations executed (custom TypeORM-style runner with `migrations` table tracking)
- **Tables**: 22 total (core: Users, TenantAdmins, Properties, PropertyOwners, PropertyNfcCards, VisitorPasses, VisitorLogs, VisitorDeliveries, AuditLogs, TenantMoveConfig, etc.)
- **Auth**: Dual system - Owners (email+DNI, scoped to Property) vs TenantAdmins (separate credentials, created by Superadmin)
- **Scripts**: `npm run migrate`, `npm run verify`, `npm run migrate:rollback`
- **Infra**: Proxmox + AlmaLinux 9, Nginx Proxy Manager, Cloudflare (no Docker)
</environment>

<completed_tasks>
- **Chapter 0 - Database Foundations**: Migration system, TenantAdmins table, DNI field in Users, AuditLogs, VisitorDeliveries
- **Chapter 1 - Authentication**: Email service (nodemailer), invitation flow (token-based), password reset, three-tier login (Owner/TenantAdmin/Superadmin), middleware (requireOwner, requireTenantAdmin)
- **Chapter 2 - Owner Panel**: Dashboard with property selector, requests module, consultations voting (1 unit = 1 vote), visitor management (frequent/one-time), invoice placeholder
- **Chapter 3 - Configurable Requests**: Dynamic form schemas (JSON), TenantMoveConfig (move days/rules), RequestAttachments, 5 predef request types (Mudanza, Sugerencia, Reclamo, PQR)
- **Verification**: `npm run verify` confirms 22 tables, all indexes, column integrity
</completed_tasks>

<active_issues>
- **Chapter 4 In Progress**: Creating SecurityController, vigilance UI (tablet-optimized), NFC card linking logic
- **NFC Relationship**: Must enforce 1 Card → 1 Property (not 1 Owner). Properties can have multiple cards. Cards are scoped to Tenant (condominium) to prevent cross-usage.
- **Pending**: Views for vigilance (entry/exit), delivery registration, move validation interface
</active_issues>

<file>
src/models/PropertyNfcCardModel.js

**Summary:**
Manages NFC card bindings to properties (not owners). Supports WebNFC UID scanning.

**Key elements:**
- `create(propertyId, cardUid, tenantId)` - Bind card to unit
- `validateAccess(cardUid, tenantId)` - Verify card belongs to property in this tenant
- `findByProperty(propertyId)` - Get all cards for a unit
- `deactivate(cardId)` - Disable card

**Latest version:**
```javascript
static async create(data) {
    const { property_id, card_uid, tenant_id, created_by } = data;
    const pool = await connectDB();
    const result = await pool.request()
        .input('property_id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, property_id)
        .input('card_uid', sql.NVarChar, card_uid)
        .input('tenant_id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, tenant_id)
        .input('created_by', sql.UniqueIdentifier, created_by)
        .query(`
            INSERT INTO PropertyNfcCards (property_id, card_uid, tenant_id, created_by, status)
            OUTPUT INSERTED.*
            VALUES (@property_id, @card_uid, @tenant_id, @created_by, 'ACTIVE')
        `);
    return result.recordset[0];
}
```
</file>

<file>
src/migrations/202503160001-CreatePropertyNfcCards.js

**Summary:**
Creates NFC card table with property relationship (not user relationship).

**Key elements:**
- `card_uid` (unique per tenant)
- `property_id` (FK to Properties)
- `status`: ACTIVE/INACTIVE/LOST
- Constraint: Unique card_uid per tenant

**Latest version:**
```sql
CREATE TABLE PropertyNfcCards (
    id UNIQUEIDENTIFIER PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT NEWID(),
    tenant_id UNIQUEIDENTIFIER NOT NULL,
    property_id UNIQUEIDENTIFIER NOT NULL,
    card_uid NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    status NVARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
    created_by UNIQUEIDENTIFIER NOT NULL,
    created_at DATETIME2 DEFAULT SYSDATETIME(),
    CONSTRAINT UQ_PropertyNfcCards_CardUid_PerTenant UNIQUE (tenant_id, card_uid)
);
```
</file>

<file>
src/controllers/SecurityController.js

**Summary:**
Vigilance/Security portal controller for guard access. Tablet-optimized responses.

**Key elements:**
- `validateVisitorPass()` - Check DNI against VisitorPasses
- `registerEntry()/registerExit()` - Log visitor access
- `registerDelivery()` - Quick delivery registration
- `validateNfcAccess()` - Check NFC card against PropertyNfcCards
- `getActiveVisits()` - Who is currently inside
- `getTodayMoveRequests()` - Approved moves for today

**Key logic:**
```javascript
// Validate NFC - must check card belongs to property AND property is in this tenant
static async validateNfcAccess(req, res) {
    const { card_uid } = req.body;
    const tenantId = req.user.tenantId; // From SECURITY role token
    
    const card = await PropertyNfcCardModel.findByCardUid(card_uid, tenantId);
    if (!card || card.status !== 'ACTIVE') {
        return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Tarjeta no válida' });
    }
    
    // Get property details for display
    const property = await PropertyModel.findById(card.property_id, tenantId);
    const owners = await PropertyModel.getOwners(card.property_id);
    
    return {
        valid: true,
        property: { name: property.name, building: property.building },
        owners: owners.map(o => `${o.first_name} ${o.last_name}`),
        access_granted: true
    };
}
```
</file>

- `requireTenantAdmin` - Ensures TenantAdmin login (separate from Owner)
- `requireSecurity` - New role for vigilance staff
- `requireTenantAccess` - Validates user belongs to tenant

**Latest version:**
```javascript
const requireOwner = (req, res, next) => {
    if (!req.user || req.user.type !== 'OWNER') {
        return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Propietario requerido' });
    }
    next();
};

const requireSecurity = (req, res, next) => {
    if (!req.user || req.user.role !== 'SECURITY') {
        return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Acceso de seguridad requerido' });
    }
    next();
};
```

<file>
src/services/AuthService.js

**Summary:**
Handles three authentication flows: Owner (property-scoped), TenantAdmin (tenant-scoped), Superadmin (global).

**Key elements:**
- `loginOwner()` - Returns available properties, requires property selection
- `selectProperty()` - Issues new JWT scoped to specific property
- `loginTenantAdmin()` - Separate credential check against TenantAdmins table
- `createPasswordReset()` - 1-hour expiration tokens

**Key logic:**
```javascript
// Owner login returns list of properties, not full access
const loginOwner = async (email, password) => {
    const user = await UserModel.findByEmail(email);
    // Verify password...
    const properties = await PropertyModel.getByOwner(user.id);
    return {
        token: initialToken, // No property_id yet
        properties,
        requiresPropertySelection: properties.length > 1
    };
};
```

</code_state>

<important_context>
- **DNI is Primary Key**: Users identified by Venezuelan ID (cédula), not email. Email can change, DNI cannot.
- **Multi-Property Owners**: One owner can have multiple units across different tenants (condominiums). Each unit can have up to 3 owners (Venezuelan law).
- **Billing**: Per unit (property), not per owner. Debts attach to the unit within its tenant.
- **Voting**: 1 Unit = 1 Vote. First owner to vote locks the unit. No quorum requirements, simple majority.
- **CSV Onboarding**: Superadmin uploads CSV with units + owners. System reuses existing users by DNI (no duplicates).
- **NFC Scope**: Cards are tied to Property + Tenant. A card from "Condo A" cannot work in "Condo B" even if same owner.
- **TenantAdmin vs Owner**: TenantAdmins (Junta) are created by Superadmin with separate credentials. They manage the condo but may also be owners (dual identity, separate logins).
- **Migration Strategy**: TypeORM-style with `up()`/`down()` methods, tracked in `migrations` table with batch numbers for rollback.
- **Visitor Logic**: Frequent visits have no expiration (toggle on/off). One-time visits have specific date.
</important_context>

Se requiere rol de seguridad.' });\n};\n\nrouter.use(authenticate, requireSecurity);\n\n// ==================== DASHBOARD ====================\nrouter.get('/dashboard', SecurityController.getDashboard);\n\n// ==================== CONTROL DE ACCESO NFC ====================\nrouter.post('/nfc/validate', SecurityController.validateNFC);\nrouter.get('/nfc/recent-logs', SecurityController.getRecentLogs);\n\n// ==================== VISITAS ====================\nrouter.get('/visits/today', SecurityController.getTodayVisits);\nrouter.post('/visits/:id/entry', SecurityController.registerVisitEntry);\nrouter.post('/visits/:id/exit', SecurityController.registerVisitExit);\n\n// ==================== DELIVERIES ====================\nrouter.get('/deliveries', SecurityController.getDeliveries);\nrouter.post('/deliveries', SecurityController.createDelivery);\nrouter.put('/deliveries/:id/deliver', SecurityController.deliverPackage);\n\n// ==================== MUDANZAS ====================\nrouter.get('/moves/today', SecurityController.getTodayMoves);\n\nmodule.exports = router;\n"

'<div class=\"p-4 text-center text-slate-500 text-sm\">No hay mudanzas aprobadas para hoy</div>';\n        return;\n    }\n\n    container.innerHTML = moves.map(move => `\n        <div class=\"p-3 {\n    const container = document.getElementById('activity-list');\n    if (logs.length === 0) {\n        container.innerHTML = '<div class=\"p-4 text-center text-slate-500 text-sm\">Sin actividad reciente</div>';\n        return;\n    }\n\n    container.innerHTML = logs.map(log => `\n        <div class=\"p-3 flex items-center gap-3\">\n            <div class=\"w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex <div class=\"font-medium truncate\">${log.property_name || 'Acceso'}</div>\n                <div class=\"text-xs text-slate-400\">${timeAgo(log.access_time)}</div>\n            </div>\n        </div>\n    `).join('');\n}\n\nfunction getAccessStatusColor(status) {\n    switch(status) {\n        case 'GRANTED': return 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400';\n        case 'DENIED': return timeAgo(date) {\n    const seconds = Math.floor((new Date() - new Date(date)) / 1000);\n    if (seconds < 60) return 'ahora';\n    const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);\n    if (minutes < 60) document.getElementById('delivery-company').value,\n        notes: document.getElementById('delivery-notes').value\n    };\n\n    try {\n        const res = await fetch('/api/security/deliveries', {\n function manualNfcCheck() {\n    const uid = document.getElementById('nfc-input').value.trim();\n    if (!uid) return;\n\n    try {\n        const res = await fetch('/api/security/nfc/validate', {\n    440	                .input('tenant_id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, tenantId)
   441	                .query(`
   442	                    UPDATE VisitorDeliveries 
   443	                    SET status = 'DELIVERED', 
   444	                        delivered_at = SYSDATETIME(),
   445	                        updated_at = SYSDATETIME()
   446	                    OUTPUT INSERTED.*
   447	                    WHERE id = @id AND tenant_id = @tenant_id
   448	                `);
   449	
   450	            if (result.recordset.length === 0) {
   451	                return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Delivery no encontrado' });
   452	            }
   453	
   454	            res.json({
   455	                success: true,
   456	                message: 'Delivery marcado como entregado',
   457	                data: result.recordset[0]
   458	            });
   459	        } catch (error) {
   460	            console.error('Deliver package error:', error);
   461	            res.status(500).json({ error: 'Error al actualizar delivery' });
   462	        }
   463	    }

   465	    // ==================== MUDANZAS ====================
   466	
   467	    /**
   468	     * GET /api/security/moves/today
   469	     * Mudanzas aprobadas para hoy
   470	     */
   471	    static async getTodayMoves(req, res) {
   472	        try {
   473	            const tenantId = req.user.tenantId;
   474	            const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
   475	
   476	            const pool = await connectDB();
   477	            const result = await pool.request()
   478	                .input('tenant_id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, tenantId)
   479	                .input('today', sql.Date, today)
   480	                .query(`
   481	                    SELECT r.id, r.title, r.description, r.data, r.status,
   482	                           r.created_at, p.name as property_name, p.building,
   483	                           u.first_name + ' ' + u.last_name as owner_name,
   484	                           u.phone as owner_phone
   485	                    FROM Requests r
   486	                    INNER JOIN RequestTypes rt ON r.request_type_id = rt.id
   487	                    INNER JOIN Properties p ON r.property_id = p.id
   488	                    INNER JOIN Users u ON r.user_id = u.id
   489	                    WHERE r.tenant_id = @tenant_id
   490	                    AND rt.name LIKE '%Mudanza%'
   491	                    AND r.status IN ('OPEN', 'IN_PROGRESS')
   492	                    AND r.data LIKE '%' + @today + '%'
   493	                    ORDER BY r.created_at ASC
   494	                `);
   495	
   496	            // Parsear data JSON
   497	            const moves = result.recordset.map(m => ({
   498	                ...m,
   499	                data: m.data ? JSON.parse(m.data) : null
   500	            }));
   501	
   502	            res.json({
   503	                success: true,
   504	                data: moves
   505	            });
   506	        } catch (error) {
   507	            console.error('Get today moves error:', error);
   508	            res.status(500).json({ error: 'Error al obtener mudanzas' });
   509	        }
   510	    }
   511	}
   512	
   513	module.exports = SecurityController;

   119	    static async update(id, tenantId, data) {
   120	        const allowedFields = ['card_name', 'description', 'is_active', 'is_blocked', 'block_reason', 'expires_at'];
   121	        const updates = [];
   122	        
   123	        for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(data)) {
   124	            if (allowedFields.includes(key)) {
   125	                updates.push(`${key} = @${key}`);
   126	            }
   127	        }
   128	
   129	        if (updates.length === 0) return null;
   130	
   131	        try {
   132	            const pool = await connectDB();
   133	            const request = pool.request()
   134	                .input('id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, id)
   135	                .input('tenant_id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, tenantId);
   136	
   137	            for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(data)) {
   138	                if (allowedFields.includes(key)) {
   139	                    if (key === 'is_active' || key === 'is_blocked') {
   140	                        request.input(key, sql.Bit, value ? 1 : 0);
   141	                    } else if (key === 'expires_at') {
   142	                        request.input(key, sql.DateTime2, value);
   143	                    } else {
   144	                        request.input(key, sql.NVarChar, value);
   145	                    }
   146	                }
   147	            }
   148	
   149	            const result = await request.query(`
   150	                UPDATE NFC_Cards 
   151	                SET ${updates.join(', ')}, updated_at = SYSDATETIME()
   152	                OUTPUT INSERTED.*
   153	                WHERE id = @id AND tenant_id = @tenant_id
   154	            `);
   155	
   156	            return result.recordset[0] || null;
   157	        } catch (error) {
   158	            console.error('Error updating NFC card:', error);
   159	            throw error;
   160	        }
   161	    }

   193	    static async validateCard(cardUid, tenantId) {
   194	        try {
   195	            const card = await this.findByUid(cardUid, tenantId);
   196	
   197	            if (!card) {
   198	                return {
   199	                    valid: false,

   201	                    reason: 'CARD_NOT_FOUND',
   202	                    message: 'Tarjeta no registrada'

   205	
   206	            if (!card.is_active) {
   207	                return {
   208	                    valid: false,

   210	                    reason: 'CARD_INACTIVE',
   211	                    message: 'Tarjeta inactiva'
   212	                };
   213	            }
   214	
   215	            if (card.is_blocked) {
   216	                return {
   217	                    valid: false,
   218	                    card,
   219	                    reason: 'CARD_BLOCKED',
   220	                    message: card.block_reason || 'Tarjeta bloqueada'
   221	                };
   222	            }
   223	
   224	            if (card.expires_at && new Date(card.expires_at) < new Date()) {
   225	                return {
   226	                    valid: false,
   227	                    card,
   228	                    reason: 'CARD_EXPIRED',
   229	                    message: 'Tarjeta expirada'
   230	                };
   231	            }
   232	
   233	            // Verificar que el propietario de la unidad esté activo
   234	            const pool = await connectDB();
   235	            const ownerResult = await pool.request()
   236	                .input('property_id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, card.property_id)
   237	                .query(`
   238	                    SELECT TOP 1 u.first_name, u.last_name, u.is_active

   241	                    WHERE po.property_id = @property_id AND po.is_primary_owner = 1
   242	                `);
   243	
   244	            if (ownerResult.recordset.length === 0) {
   245	                return {
   246	                    valid: false,
   247	                    card,
   248	                    reason: 'NO_OWNER',
   249	                    message: 'Unidad sin propietario registrado'
   250	                };
   251	            }
   252	
   253	            const owner = ownerResult.recordset[0];
   254	            if (!owner.is_active) {
   255	                return {
   256	                    valid: false,
   257	                    card,
   258	                    reason: 'OWNER_INACTIVE',
   259	                    message: 'Propietario inactivo'
   260	                };
   261	            }
   262	
   263	            return {
   264	                valid: true,
   265	                card: {
   266	                    ...card,
   267	                    owner_name: `${owner.first_name} ${owner.last_name}`
   268	                },
   269	                reason: null,

   322

   341	    /**
   342	     * Obtener logs de acceso de un tenant
   343	     * @param {string} tenantId 
   344	     * @param {Object} options - { limit, offset, startDate, endDate, status }
   345	     */
   346	    static async getAccessLogs(tenantId, options = {}) {
   347	        const { limit = 50, offset = 0, startDate = null, endDate = null, status = null } = options;
   348	
   349	        try {
   350	            let query = `
   351	                SELECT l.*, u.first_name + ' ' + u.last_name as registered_by_name
   352	                FROM NFC_AccessLogs l
   353	                LEFT JOIN Users u ON l.registered_by = u.id
   354	                WHERE l.tenant_id = @tenant_id
   355	            `;
   356	
   357	            const request = pool.request()
   358	                .input('tenant_id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, tenantId)
   359	                .input('limit', sql.Int, limit)
   360	                .input('offset', sql.Int, offset);
   361	
   362	            if (startDate) {
   363	                query += ` AND l.access_time >= @startDate`;
   364	                request.input('startDate', sql.DateTime2, startDate);
   365	            }
   366	
   367	            if (endDate) {
   368	                query += ` AND l.access_time <= @endDate`;
   369	                request.input('endDate', sql.DateTime2, endDate);
   370	            }
   371	
   372	            if (status) {
   373	                query += ` AND l.status = @status`;
   374	                request.input('status', sql.NVarChar, status);
   375	            }
   376	
   377	            query += ` ORDER BY l.access_time DESC OFFSET @offset ROWS FETCH NEXT @limit ROWS ONLY`;
   378	
   379	            const pool = await connectDB();
   380	            const result = await request.query(query);
   381	
   382	            return result.recordset;
   383	        } catch (error) {
   384	            console.error('Error getting access logs:', error);
   385	            throw error;
   386	        }
   387	    }

   404	                        SUM(CASE WHEN is_blocked = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as blocked
   405	                    FROM NFC_Cards
   406	                    WHERE tenant_id = @tenant_id
   407	                `);
   408	
   409	            // Accesos de hoy
   410	            const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];

   413	                .input('today', sql.Date, today)
   414	                .query(`
   415	                    SELECT 
   416	                        COUNT(*) as total,
   417	                        SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'GRANTED' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as granted,
   418	                        SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'DENIED' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as denied
   419	                    FROM NFC_AccessLogs
   420	                    WHERE tenant_id = @tenant_id AND CAST(access_time AS DATE) = @today
   421	                `);
   422	
   423	            return {
   424	                cards: cardsResult.recordset[0],
   425	                todayAccess: accessResult.recordset[0]
   426	            };
   427	        } catch (error) {
   428	            console.error('Error getting NFC stats:', error);
   429	            throw error;
   430	        }

    55	                .input('alias', sql.NVarChar, alias || null)
    56	                .input('valid_from', sql.DateTime2, valid_from || new Date())
    57	                .input('valid_until', sql.DateTime2, valid_until || null)
    58	                .query(`
    59	                    INSERT INTO VisitorPasses (tenant_id, visitor_id, user_id, property_id, type, alias, valid_from, valid_until)
    60	                    OUTPUT INSERTED.*
    61	                    VALUES (@tenant_id, @visitor_id, @user_id, @property_id, @type, @alias, @valid_from, @valid_until)
    62	                `);
    63	            return result.recordset[0];
    64	        } catch (error) {
    65	            console.error('Error creating visitor pass:', error);
    66	            throw error;
    67	        }
    68	    }
    69	
    70	    /**
    71	     * Find valid pass for a visitor
    72	     */
    73	    static async findValidPass(tenantId, visitorId) {
    74	        try {
    75	            const pool = await connectDB();
    76	            // Looks for active passes that are either FREQUENT (and active) or ONE_TIME (and within date range)
    77	            const result = await pool.request()
    78	                .input('tenant_id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, tenantId)
    79	                .input('visitor_id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, visitorId)
    80	                .query(`
    81	                    SELECT TOP 1 * FROM VisitorPasses
    82	                    WHERE tenant_id = @tenant_id 
    83	                    AND visitor_id = @visitor_id
    84	                    AND status = 'ACTIVE'
    85	                    AND (
    86	                        (type = 'FREQUENT') OR 
    87	                        (type = 'ONE_TIME' AND CAST(valid_from AS DATE) <= CAST(SYSDATETIME() AS DATE) AND (valid_until IS NULL OR CAST(valid_until AS DATE) >= CAST(SYSDATETIME() AS DATE)))
    88	                    )
    89	                    ORDER BY created_at DESC
    90	                `);
    91	            return result.recordset[0] || null;
    92	        } catch (error) {
    93	            console.error('Error finding valid pass:', error);
    94	            throw error;
    95	        }
    96	    }
    97	
    98	    /**
    99	     * Search visitors by DNI or Name within a tenant
   100	     */
   101	    static async search(tenantId, query) {
   102	        try {
   103	            const pool = await connectDB();
   104	            const result = await pool.request()
   105	                .input('tenant_id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, tenantId)
   106	                .input('query', sql.NVarChar, `%${query}%`)
   107	                .query(`
   108	                    SELECT * FROM Visitors 
   109	                    WHERE tenant_id = @tenant_id 
   110	                    AND (dni LIKE @query OR first_name LIKE @query OR last_name LIKE @query)
   111	                    ORDER BY created_at DESC
   112	                `);
   113	            return result.recordset;
   114	        } catch (error) {
   115	            console.error('Error searching visitors:', error);
   116	            throw error;
   117	        }
   118	    }
   119	
   120	    /**
   121	     * Log Entry
   122	     */
   123	    static async logEntry(data) {
   124	        const { tenant_id, visitor_id, pass_id, property_id, user_id, access_method, vehicle_plate, notes } = data;
   125	        try {
   126	            const pool = await connectDB();
   127	            const result = await pool.request()
   128	                .input('tenant_id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, tenant_id)
   129	                .input('visitor_id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, visitor_id)
   130	                .input('pass_id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, pass_id || null)
   131	                .input('property_id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, property_id || null)
   132	                .input('user_id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, user_id || null)
   133	                .input('access_method', sql.NVarChar, access_method || 'PEDESTRIAN')
   134	                .input('vehicle_plate', sql.NVarChar, vehicle_plate || null)
   135	                .input('notes', sql.NVarChar, notes || null)
   136	                .query(`
   137	                    INSERT INTO VisitorLogs (tenant_id, visitor_id, pass_id, property_id, user_id, access_method, vehicle_plate, notes, entry_time)
   138	                    OUTPUT INSERTED.*
   139	                    VALUES (@tenant_id, @visitor_id, @pass_id, @property_id, @user_id, @access_method, @vehicle_plate, @notes, SYSDATETIME())
   140	                `);
   141	            return result.recordset[0];
   142	        } catch (error) {
   143	            console.error('Error logging visit entry:', error);
   144	            throw error;
   145	        }
   146	    }
   147	
   148	    /**
   149	     * Log exit
   150	     */
   151	    static async logExit(visitId, tenantId) {
   152	        try {
   153	            const pool = await connectDB();
   154	            const result = await pool.request()
   155	                .input('id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, visitId)
   156	                .input('tenant_id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, tenantId)
   157	                .query(`
   158	                    UPDATE VisitorLogs 
   159	                    SET exit_time = SYSDATETIME()
   160	                    OUTPUT INSERTED.*
   161	                    WHERE id = @id AND tenant_id = @tenant_id AND exit_time IS NULL
   162	                `);
   163	            return result.recordset[0];
   164	        } catch (error) {
   165	            console.error('Error logging visit exit:', error);
   166	            throw error;
   167	        }
   168	    }
   169	
   170	    /**
   171	     * Get active visits (not exited yet)
   172	     */
   173	    static async getActiveVisits(tenantId) {
   174	        const pool = await connectDB(); // Ensure pool is obtained
   175	        try {
   176	            const result = await pool.request()
   177	                .input('tenant_id', sql.UniqueIdentifier, tenantId)
   178	                .query(`
   179	                    SELECT vl.*, 
   180	                           v.first_name, v.last_name, v.dni, v.photo_url,
   181	                           vp.type as pass_type, vp.alias as pass_alias,
   182	                           p.name as property_name,
   183	                           u.first_name as auth_user_first_name, u.last_name as auth_user_last_name
   184	                    FROM VisitorLogs vl
   185	                    INNER JOIN Visitors v ON vl.visitor_id = v.id
   186	                    LEFT JOIN VisitorPasses vp ON vl.pass_id = vp.id
   187	                    LEFT JOIN Properties p ON vl.property_id = p.id
   188	                    LEFT JOIN Users u ON vl.user_id = u.id
   189	                    WHERE vl.tenant_id = @tenant_id AND vl.exit_time IS NULL
   190	                    ORDER BY vl.entry_time DESC
   191	                `);
   192	            return result.recordset;
   193	        } catch (error) {
   194	            console.error('Error fetching active visits:', error);
   195	            throw error;
   196	        }
   197	    }
   198	}

Excelente. **Chapter 4: Visitor Control & NFC Access System** completado exitosamente.

## Resumen de implementación:

### 1. Base de Datos (2 nuevas tablas)
- **NFC_Cards**: Almacena tarjetas NFC vinculadas a Properties (no a Owners)
  - `card_uid` + `tenant_id` únicos (previene uso cruzado entre condominios)
  - Estados: `is_active`, `is_blocked`, `expires_at`
  - Relación: 1 Card = 1 Property, 1 Property puede tener N Cards

- **NFC_AccessLogs**: Registro de accesos con validación
  - Guarda éxitos y rechazos con razones
  - Soporte para ENTRY/EXIT

### 2. Backend API
**SecurityController** (`/api/security/*`):
- `GET /dashboard` - Stats para vigilancia
- `POST /nfc/validate` - Validar tarjeta y registrar acceso
- `GET /nfc/recent-logs` - Últimos accesos
- `GET /visits/today` - Visitas esperadas hoy
- `POST /visits/:id/entry|exit` - Registrar entrada/salida
- `GET|POST /deliveries` - Gestión de paquetes
- `GET /moves/today` - Mudanzas aprobadas para hoy

**NFCAdminController** (`/api/tenant-admin/nfc/*`):
- CRUD completo de tarjetas NFC
- Bloqueo/desbloqueo de tarjetas
- Estadísticas y logs de acceso

### 3. Frontend (Tablet-optimized)
**Panel de Seguridad** (`/security`):
- UI diseñada para tablets (touch targets grandes)
- 4 botones principales: NFC, Visita, Paquete, Visitas Activas
- Dashboard en tiempo real con stats
- Modales para cada operación
- Esquema de colores oscuro (ideal para uso prolongado)

### 4. Correcciones
- Fixed auth middleware en todas las rutas (`authMiddleware` → `authenticate`)
- Actualizado script de verificación para incluir tablas NFC

### Estado final:
- ✅ 24 tablas en base de datos
- ✅ 12 migraciones ejecutadas
- ✅ App carga sin errores
- ✅ APIs de seguridad listas para integración con frontend

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

El usuario me pregunta si debería continuar con los siguientes capítulos o abordar el landing y el panel de super admin. Según el contexto del proyecto, ya he completado:

- Chapter 0: Database Foundations
- Chapter 1: Authentication 
- Chapter 2: Owner Panel
- Chapter 3: Configurable Requests
- Chapter 4: Visitor Control & NFC Access System

Normalmente el siguiente paso sería Chapter 5, pero el usuario también está sugiriendo hacer el landing y panel de super admin primero. 

El landing es importante para el SaaS (marketing site), y el panel de super admin es necesario para gestionar tenants. Ambos son componentes importantes para tener un sistema funcional completo.

Dado que:
1. Ya tenemos el sistema core funcionando (autenticación, propietarios, solicitudes, seguridad)
2. El super admin es necesario para gestionar tenants (onboarding de nuevos condominios)
3. El landing es la cara visible del SaaS

Creo que tiene sentido hacer el landing y super admin ahora para tener un producto "completo" desde el punto de vista de administración SaaS. Luego podemos volver a los capítulos adicionales.

Pero debería preguntarle al usuario qué prefiere, ya que él está preguntando mi opinión. Voy a sugerir hacer el landing y super admin primero porque:
1. Permite onboarding de nuevos tenants
2. Completa la experiencia de administración SaaS
3. Es más fácil de implementar que los siguientes capítulos complejos

Pero también puedo preguntarle cuáles son los siguientes capítulos que tiene planeado para poder dar una mejor recomendación.

</details>

¡Buena pregunta! Depende de tus prioridades:

**Opción A: Landing + Super Admin (mi recomendación)**
- Completa el flujo SaaS: landing de marketing → onboarding de tenants → gestión
- El Super Admin es necesario para crear nuevos condominios (tenants)
- Es más rápido de implementar y da un producto "completo" para demo/ventas
- El landing ayuda con la presentación del producto

**Opción B: Continuar con Chapter 5+**
- Si tienes capítulos definidos (¿Cuáles serían? ¿Facturación? ¿Comunicaciones? ¿Reservas de áreas comunes?)
- Agrega más funcionalidad al core del condominio
- Pero sin Super Admin, no hay forma de crear nuevos tenants fácilmente

**Mi recomendación:** Hagamos el **Landing + Super Admin Panel** primero porque:
1. Necesitas el Super Admin para gestionar tenants (crear condominios, asignar admins)
2. El landing es la cara visible del SaaS
3. Luego podemos seguir con más features del core

¿Qué prefieres? ¿Y cuáles serían los siguientes capítulos que tienes planeados? (Así puedo priorizar mejor)

     1	const express = require('express');
     2	const router = express.Router();
     3	const AdminController = require('../controllers/AdminController');
     4	
     5	// Middleware to ensure user is SuperAdmin
     6	const { authenticate } = require('../middleware/auth');
     7	
     8	const verifySuperAdmin = (req, res, next) => {
     9	    if (!req.user || !req.user.isSuperAdmin) {
    10	        return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Access denied. Super Admin only.' });
    11	    }
    12	    next();

     1	const TenantModel = require('../models/TenantModel');
     2	const UserModel = require('../models/UserModel');
     3	const { sql, connectDB } = require('../config/database');
     4	
     5	class AdminController {
     6	    /**
     7	     * Get system-wide statistics
     8	     */
     9	    static async getStats(req, res) {
    10	        try {
    11	            const pool = await connectDB();
    12	
    13	            // Count Tenants
    14	            const tenantsResult = await pool.request().query('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM Tenants');
    15	            const tenantsCount = tenantsResult.recordset[0].count;
    16	
    17	            // Count Users
    18	            const usersResult = await pool.request().query('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM Users');
    19	            const usersCount = usersResult.recordset[0].count;
    20	
    21	            // TODO: Calculate monthly revenue if billing module exists
    22	            const revenue = 0;
    23	
    24	            res.json({
    25	                tenants: tenantsCount,
    26	                users: usersCount,
    27	                revenue: revenue
    28	            });
    29	        } catch (error) {
    30	            console.error('Error fetching admin stats:', error);
    31	            res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal Server Error' });
    32	        }
    33	    }
    34	
    35	    /**
    36	     * List all tenants
    37	     */
    38	    static async getAllTenants(req, res) {
    39	        try {
    40	            const pool = await connectDB();
    41	            const result = await pool.request().query('SELECT * FROM Tenants ORDER BY created_at DESC');
    42	            res.json(result.recordset);
    43	        } catch (error) {
    44	            console.error('Error fetching tenants:', error);
    45	            res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal Server Error' });
    46	        }
    47	    }
    48	
    49	    /**
    50	     * Create a new tenant
    51	     */
    52	    static async createTenant(req, res) {
    53	        try {
    54	            const tenantData = req.body;
    55	            // Basic validation
    56	            if (!tenantData.name || !tenantData.slug) {
    57	                return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Name and Slug are required' });
    58	            }
    59	
    60	            const newTenant = await TenantModel.create(tenantData);
    61	            res.status(201).json(newTenant);
    62	        } catch (error) {
    63	            if (error.code === 'EREQUEST' && error.message.includes('UNIQUE KEY')) {
    64	                return res.status(409).json({ error: 'Tenant slug already exists' });
    65	            }
    66	            res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal Server Error' });
    67	        }
    68	    }
    69	}
    70	
    71	module.exports = AdminController;

   149	
   150	            async function fetchTenants() {
   151	                try {

   153	                        headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
   154	                    });
   155	                    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch tenants');
   156	                    const tenants = await res.json();
   157

   171	                            <td class="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap"><span class="px-2 inline-flex text-xs leading-5 font-semibold rounded-full bg-green-100 text-green-800">${t.active ? 'Activo' : 'Inactivo'}</span></td>
   172	                            <td class="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-right text-sm font-medium"><a href="#" class="text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-900">Editar</a></td>
   173	                        </tr>
   174	                    `).join('');

   195	    static async update(id, data) {
   196	        const allowedFields = ['first_name', 'last_name', 'email', 'phone', 'avatar_url', 'is_active'];
   197	        const updates = [];
   198	        
   199	        for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(data)) {
   200	            if (allowedFields.includes(key)) {
   201	                updates.push(`${key} = @${key}`);
   202	            }
   203	        }
   204	
   205	        if (updates.length === 0) return null;
   206

   210

   213	                if (allowedFields.includes(key)) {

   215	                }
   216	            }
   217

   221	                OUTPUT INSERTED.*

     7	class AuthController {

    11	    /**
    12	     * POST /api/auth/login
    13	     * Login unificado - detecta tipo de usuario automáticamente

    15	    static async login(req, res) {
    16	        try {
    17	            const { email, password, type } = req.body;

    19	            if (!email || !password) {

    30	            } else if (type === 'SUPERADMIN') {
    31	                result = await AuthService.loginSuperAdmin(email, password);
    32	            } else {
    33	                // Por defecto, intentar como propietario primero
    34	                try {
    35	                    result = await AuthService.loginOwner(email, password);

    55	            console.error('Login error:', error);

    87	            });
    88	        }

    91	    /**
    92	     * POST /api/auth/login/admin
    93	     * Login específico para admins de junta
    94	     */
    95	    static async loginTenantAdmin(req, res) {
    96	        try {
    97	            const { email, password } = req.body;
    98	
    99	            if (!email || !password) {
   100	                return res.status(400).json({ 
   101	                    error: 'Email y contraseña son requeridos' 
   102	                });
   103	            }

   106	
   107	            res.json({
   108	                success: true,
   109	                ...result
   110	            });
   111	
   112	        } catch (error) {
   113	            console.error('Admin login error:', error);
   114	            res.status(401).json({ 
   115	                error: error.message || 'Error de autenticación' 
   116	            });
   117	        }
   118	    }

   122	     * Login para superadmin
   123	     */
   124	    static async loginSuperAdmin(req, res) {

   127

   131	                });
   132	            }
   133

   135	
   136	            res.json({

   138	                ...result
   139	            });
   140	
   141	        } catch (error) {

## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Landing + Super Admin Panel

## Contexto Actual

### Landing Existente
- **Archivo**: `src/views/landing.ejs`
- **Estado**: Básico pero funcional (Hero, 3 Features, CTA)
- **Problemas**: 
  - Links rotos ("Comenzar Ahora" apunta a #)
  - Sin pricing/planes
  - Sin formulario de contacto/demo
  - Sin testimonios/social proof
  - Sin FAQ
  - Footer incompleto

### Super Admin Existente
- **API**: `/api/admin/*` con stats y tenants
- **Vista**: `src/views/admin/dashboard.ejs` básico
- **Modelos**: TenantModel, UserModel funcionales
- **Problemas**:
  - Dashboard sin datos dinámicos reales
  - No hay modal/formulario para crear tenants
  - No hay gestión de admins de junta por tenant
  - No hay onboarding wizard para nuevos tenants
  - Sin auditoría/actividad global

## Objetivos

### Landing (Marketing Site)
1. Sección Hero mejorada con CTA claro (Solicitar Demo)
2. Sección Features expandida (6+ características)
3. Sección Pricing con 3 planes
4. Sección Testimonios/Social Proof
5. Sección FAQ
6. Formulario de Contacto/Demo
7. Footer completo con links

### Super Admin Panel
1. Dashboard con métricas reales (KPIs)
2. CRUD completo de Tenants (Condominios)
3. Onboarding Wizard para nuevos tenants:
   - Paso 1: Datos del condominio
   - Paso 2: Crear admin de junta principal
   - Paso 3: Configuración inicial
4. Gestión de TenantAdmins por tenant
5. Auditoría global (última actividad)
6. Configuración global (tasas BCV, etc.)

## Arquitectura

### Landing
```
GET /          → landing.ejs (mejorado)
GET /contact   → contact.ejs (formulario)
GET /pricing   → pricing.ejs (planes)
```

### Super Admin
```
GET  /admin                 → dashboard mejorado
GET  /api/admin/stats       → métricas ampliadas
GET  /api/admin/tenants     → listado con filtros
POST /api/admin/tenants     → crear tenant
PUT  /api/admin/tenants/:id → editar tenant
GET  /api/admin/tenants/:id/admins → admins del tenant
POST /api/admin/onboarding  → wizard completo
GET  /api/admin/audit-logs  → actividad global
```

## Cambios a Realizar

### 1. Landing Page Mejorada

**Archivos a modificar/crear:**
- `src/views/landing.ejs` - Expandir con nuevas secciones
- `src/views/partials/footer.ejs` - Footer reutilizable
- `src/views/contact.ejs` - Formulario de contacto
- `src/views/pricing.ejs` - Página de planes
- `src/routes/index.js` - Agregar rutas

**Secciones a agregar:**
1. **Hero**: Mejorar copy, agregar formulario de email para demo
2. **Social Proof**: Logos de condominios, contadores (si aplica)
3. **Features Detalladas**: 6 tarjetas con iconos
4. **How it Works**: 3 pasos simples
5. **Pricing**: 3 planes (Básico, Profesional, Empresarial)
6. **Testimonios**: 3 tarjetas de clientes
7. **FAQ**: Acordeón con preguntas frecuentes
8. **CTA Final**: Formulario de demo
9. **Footer**: Links, redes, newsletter

### 2. Super Admin Dashboard

**Archivos a modificar:**
- `src/views/admin/dashboard.ejs` - Rediseño completo
- `src/controllers/AdminController.js` - Expandir endpoints
- `src/routes/admin.js` - Agregar rutas nuevas

**Componentes del Dashboard:**
1. **KPI Cards**:
   - Total Tenants (activos/inactivos)
   - Total Users (propietarios + admins)
   - Properties totales
   - Ingresos mensuales (placeholder)
   - Tickets/Mudanzas pendientes (global)

2. **Tabla de Tenants**:
   - Filtros por estado, fecha
   - Búsqueda
   - Acciones: Ver, Editar, Suspender
   - Stats por tenant (users, properties)

3. **Modal: Nuevo Tenant (Wizard)**:
   - Step 1: Nombre, slug, dirección
   - Step 2: Datos del admin principal (nombre, email, cédula)
   - Step 3: Configuración (billing_type, settings)
   - Review y confirmación

4. **Sección Auditoría**:
   - Últimos 10 eventos globales
   - Logins, creaciones, errores

### 3. API Endpoints Nuevos/Mejorados

**AdminController:**
- `getStats()` - Agregar más métricas
- `getTenantDetails()` - Stats detalladas por tenant
- `onboardTenant()` - Wizard endpoint
- `suspendTenant()` - Activar/desactivar
- `getAuditLogs()` - Auditoría global

### 4. Modelos (revisar/actualizar)

**TenantModel:**
- Agregar `getStats(tenantId)` - contar users, properties
- Agregar `getWithStats()` - listado con métricas

**UserModel:**
- Verificar funciones para crear TenantAdmin

### 5. Base de Datos (verificar)

Tablas necesarias confirmadas:
- ✅ Tenants - Existe
- ✅ TenantAdmins - Existe
- ✅ Users - Existe
- ✅ AuditLogs - Existe

No se necesitan nuevas migraciones.

## Plan de Implementación

### Phase 1: Landing (2-3 pasos)
1. Expandir landing.ejs con nuevas secciones
2. Crear footer partial
3. Crear páginas contact y pricing

### Phase 2: Super Admin Dashboard (4-5 pasos)
1. Rediseñar dashboard.ejs con layout moderno
2. Mejorar AdminController.getStats()
3. Crear modal/wizard para nuevo tenant
4. Agregar gestión de TenantAdmins
5. Agregar auditoría global

### Phase 3: Testing & Polish
1. Verificar todas las rutas
2. Probar flujo de onboarding
3. Revisar responsive design

## Criterios de Éxito

- ✅ Landing profesional con todas las secciones
- ✅ Formulario de demo funcional (envía email o guarda lead)
- ✅ Dashboard Super Admin con datos reales
- ✅ Wizard de onboarding crea tenant + admin en una transacción
- ✅ Auditoría muestra actividad reciente
- ✅ Todo es responsive (mobile, tablet, desktop)


</details>

links\ndocument.querySelectorAll('a[href^=\"#\"]').forEach(anchor => {\n    anchor.addEventListener('click', function (e) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        const target = document.querySelector(this.getAttribute('href'));\n        if (target) {\n            target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });\n            // Close mobile menu if open\n            document.getElementById('mobile-menu').classList.add('hidden');\n        }\n    });\n});\n\n// FAQ toggle\nfunction toggleFaq(button) {\n    const content = button.nextElementSibling;\n    const arrow = button.querySelector('svg');\n    \n    content.classList.toggle('hidden');\n    arrow.classList.toggle('rotate-180');\n}\n\n// Demo request handler\nfunction handleDemoRequest(e) {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    const email = e.target.querySelector('input[type=\"email\"]').value;\n    \n    // Show coming soon message\n    alert('¡Gracias por tu interés! Te contactaremos en ' + email + ' para coordinar una demo personalizada.');\n    e.target.reset();\n}\n\n// Plan selection\nfunction selectPlan(plan) {\n    alert('Plan ' + plan + ' seleccionado. Serás redirigido al formulario de contacto.');\n    document.getElementById('demo').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });\n}\n\n// Navbar background on scroll\nwindow.addEventListener('scroll', () => {\n    const navbar = document.getElementById('navbar');\n    if (window.scrollY > 10) {\n        navbar.classList.add('shadow-sm');\n    } else {\n        navbar.classList.remove('shadow-sm');\n    }\n});\n</script>\n\n<style>\n@keyframes blob {\n    0% { transform: translate(0px, 0px) scale(1); }\n    33% { transform: translate(30px, -50px) scale(1.1); }\n    66% { transform: translate(-20px, 20px) scale(0.9); }\n    100% { transform: translate(0px, 0px) scale(1); }\n}\n.animate-blob {\n    animation: blob 7s infinite;\n}\n.animation-delay-2000 {\n    animation-delay: 2s;\n}\n</style>\n"
}

Total Users -->\n                    <div class=\"bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100\">\n                        <div class=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n                            <div>\n                                <p class=\"text-sm text-gray-500 mb-1\">Usuarios Totales</p>\n                                <h3 class=\"text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900\" id=\"stat-users\">0</h3>\n                                <p class=\"text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1\">\n                                    <span id=\"stat-owners\">0</span> propietarios\n                                </p>\n                            </div>\n                            <div class=\"w-12 h-12 bg-emerald-100 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center\">\n                                <svg class=\"w-6 h-6 text-emerald-600\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\">\n                                    <path stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" stroke-width=\"2\" d=\"M12 4.354a4 4 0 110 5.292M15 21H3v-1a6 6 0 0112 0v1zm0 0h6v-1a6 6 0 00-9-5.197M13 7a4 4 0 11-8 0 4 4 0 018 0z\"/>\n                                </svg>\n                            </div>\n                        </div>\n                    </div>\n\n                    <!-- Total Properties -->\n                    <div class=\"bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100\">\n                        <div class=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n                            <div>\n                                <p class=\"text-sm text-gray-500 mb-1\">Unidades</p>\n                                <h3 class=\"text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900\" id=\"stat-properties\">0</h3>\n                                <p class=\"text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1\">Inmobiliarias</p>\n                            </div>\n                            <div class=\"w-12 h-12 bg-amber-100 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center\">\n                                <svg class=\"w-6 h-6 text-amber-600\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\">\n                                    <path stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" stroke-width=\"2\" d=\"M3 12l2-2m0 0l7-7 7 7M5 10v10a1 1 0 001 1h3m10-11l2 2m-2-2v10a1 1 0 01-1 1h-3m-6 0a1 1 0 001-1v-4a1 1 0 011-1h2a1 1 0 011 1v4a1 1 0 001 1m-6 0h6\"/>\n                                </svg>\n                            </div>\n                        </div>\n                    </div>\n\n                    <!-- Monthly Revenue (Placeholder) -->\n                    <div class=\"bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100\">\n                        <div class=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n                            <div>\n                                <p class=\"text-sm text-gray-500 mb-1\">Ingresos Mensuales</p>\n                                <h3 class=\"text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900\" id=\"stat-revenue\">$0</h3>\n                                <p class=\"text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1\">Próximamente</p>\n                            </div>\n                            <div class=\"w-12 h-12 bg-purple-100 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center\">\n                                <svg class=\"w-6 h-6 text-purple-600\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\">\n                                    <path stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" stroke-width=\"2\" d=\"M12 8c-1.657 0-3 .895-3 2s1.343 2 3 2 3 .895 3 2-1.343 2-3 2m0-8c1.11 0 2.08.402 2.599 1M12 8V7m0 1v8m0 0v1m0-1c-1.11 0-2.08-.402-2.599-1M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z\"/>\n                                </svg>\n                            </div>\n                        </div>\n                    </div>\n                </div>\n\n                <!-- Recent Activity -->\n                <div class=\"bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100\">\n                    <div class=\"px-6 py-4 border-b border-gray-100 flex items-center justify-between\">\n                        <h3 class=\"font-semibold text-gray-900\">Actividad Reciente</h3>\n                        <button onclick=\"loadAuditLogs()\" class=\"text-sm text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-700\">Ver todo</button>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"p-6\">\n                        <div id=\"activity-list\" class=\"space-y-4\">\n                            <div class=\"text-center text-gray-500 py-4\">Cargando...</div>\n                        </div>\n                    </div>\n                </div>\n\n                <!-- Tenants Section -->\n                <div id=\"tenants\" class=\"bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100\">\n                    <div class=\"px-6 py-4 border-b border-gray-100 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4\">\n                        <div>\n                            <h3 class=\"font-semibold text-gray-900 text-lg\">Condominios</h3>\n                            <p class=\"text-sm text-gray-500\">Gestiona todos los conjuntos residenciales</p>\n                        </div>\n                        <button onclick=\"openNewTenantModal()\" class=\"bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-medium transition flex items-center gap-2\">\n                            <svg class=\"w-5 h-5\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\">\n                                <path stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" stroke-width=\"2\" d=\"M12 4v16m8-8H4\"/>\n                            </svg>\n                            Nuevo Condominio\n                        </button>\n                    </div>\n                    \n                    <!-- Filters -->\n                    <div class=\"px-6 py-4 border-b border-gray-100 bg-gray-50\">\n                        <div class=\"flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-4\">\n                            <div class=\"flex-1\">\n                                <input type=\"text\" id=\"tenant-search\" placeholder=\"Buscar condominio...\" \n                                    class=\"w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500\"\n                                    oninput=\"filterTenants()\">\n                            </div>\n                            <select id=\"tenant-filter\" onchange=\"filterTenants()\" class=\"px-4 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500\">\n                                <option value=\"all\">Todos</option>\n                                <option value=\"active\">Activos</option>\n                                <option value=\"inactive\">Inactivos</option>\n                            </select>\n                        </div>\n                    </div>\n\n                    <div class=\"overflow-x-auto\">\n                        <table class=\"w-full\">\n                            <thead class=\"bg-gray-50\">\n                                <tr>\n                                    <th class=\"px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-500 uppercase\">Condominio</th>\n                                    <th class=\"px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-500 uppercase\">Estadísticas</th>\n                                    <th class=\"px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-500 uppercase\">Estado</th>\n                                    <th class=\"px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-gray-500 uppercase\">Creado</th>\n                                    <th class=\"px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium text-gray-500 uppercase\">Acciones</th>\n                                </tr>\n                            </thead>\n                            <tbody id=\"tenants-table-body\" class=\"divide-y divide-gray-100\">\n                                <tr>\n                                    <td colspan=\"5\" class=\"px-6 py-8 text-center text-gray-500\">Cargando condominios...</td>\n                                </tr>\n                            </tbody>\n                        </table>\n                    </div>\n                </div>\n\n                <!-- Audit Logs Section -->\n                <div id=\"audit\" class=\"bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100\">\n                    <div class=\"px-6 py-4 border-b border-gray-100\">\n                        <h3 class=\"font-semibold text-gray-900 text-lg\">Auditoría Global</h3>\n                        <p class=\"text-sm text-gray-500\">Registro de actividad del sistema</p>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"p-6\">\n                        <div id=\"audit-logs-container\" class=\"space-y-3 max-h-96 overflow-y-auto\">\n                            <div class=\"text-center text-gray-500 py-4\">Cargando logs...</div>\n                        </div>\n                    </div>\n                </div>\n            </div>\n        </main>\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- New Tenant Modal (Wizard) -->\n    <div id=\"new-tenant-modal\" class=\"fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 hidden z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4\">\n        <div class=\"bg-white rounded-2xl w-full max-w-2xl max-h-[90vh] overflow-hidden flex flex-col\">\n            <!-- Modal Header -->\n            <div class=\"px-6 py-4 border-b border-gray-100 flex items-center justify-between bg-gray-50\">\n                <div>\n                    <h3 class=\"font-bold text-gray-900 text-lg\">Nuevo Condominio</h3>\n                    <p class=\"text-sm text-gray-500\">Crea un nuevo conjunto residencial</p>\n                </div>\n                <button onclick=\"closeNewTenantModal()\" class=\"text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600\">\n                    <svg class=\"w-6 h-6\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\">\n                        <path stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" stroke-width=\"2\" d=\"M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12\"/>\n                    </svg>\n                </button>\n            </div>\n\n            <!-- Wizard Steps -->\n            <div class=\"px-6 py-4 bg-gray-50 border-b border-gray-100\">\n                <div class=\"flex items-center justify-center\">\n                    <div class=\"flex items-center\">\n                        <div id=\"step-1-indicator\" class=\"w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-blue-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-semibold text-sm\">1</div>\n                        <div class=\"w-16 h-1 bg-blue-600\" id=\"step-1-line\"></div>\n                        <div id=\"step-2-indicator\" class=\"w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-gray-200 text-gray-500 flex items-center justify-center font-semibold text-sm\">2</div>\n                        <div class=\"w-16 h-1 bg-gray-200\" id=\"step-2-line\"></div>\n                        <div id=\"step-3-indicator\" class=\"w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-gray-200 text-gray-500 flex items-center justify-center font-semibold text-sm\">3</div>\n                    </div>\n                </div>\n                <div class=\"flex justify-center mt-2 gap-8 text-xs text-gray-500\">\n                    <span>Condominio</span>\n                    <span>Administrador</span>\n                    <span>Revisión</span>\n                </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <!-- Modal Body -->\n            <div class=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6\">\n                <form id=\"new-tenant-form\">\n                    <!-- Step 1: Tenant Info -->\n                    <div id=\"step-1\" class=\"space-y-4\">\n                        <div>\n                            <label class=\"block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1\">Nombre del Condominio *</label>\n                            <input type=\"text\" name=\"name\" required placeholder=\"Ej: Residencias Los Samanes\"\n                                class=\"w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500\">\n                        </div>\n                        <div>\n                            <label class=\"block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1\">Slug (identificador único) *</label>\n                            <input type=\"text\" name=\"slug\" required placeholder=\"Ej: los-samanes\"\n                                pattern=\"[a-z0-9-]+\"\n                                class=\"w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500\">\n                            <p class=\"text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1\">Solo minúsculas, números y guiones. Se usará en la URL.</p>\n                        </div>\n                        <div>\n                            <label class=\"block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1\">Dirección</label>\n                            <textarea name=\"address\" rows=\"2\" placeholder=\"Dirección completa del condominio\"\n                                class=\"w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500\"></textarea>\n                        </div>\n                    </div>\n\n                    <!-- Step 2: Admin Info -->\n                    <div id=\"step-2\" class=\"space-y-4 hidden\">\n                        <div class=\"bg-blue-50 p-4 rounded-lg mb-4\">\n                            <p class=\"text-sm text-blue-700\">Crea el primer administrador de junta para este condominio. Puedes agregar más admins más tarde.</p>\n                        </div>\n                        <div class=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-4\">\n                            <div>\n                                <label class=\"block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1\">Nombre *</label>\n                                <input type=\"text\" name=\"admin_first_name\" required\n                                    class=\"w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500\">\n                            </div>\n                            <div>\n                                <label class=\"block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1\">Apellido *</label>\n                                <input type=\"text\" name=\"admin_last_name\" required\n                                    class=\"w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500\">\n                            </div>\n                        </div>\n                        <div>\n                            <label class=\"block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1\">Email *</label>\n                            <input type=\"email\" name=\"admin_email\" required\n                                class=\"w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500\">\n                        </div>\n                        <div>\n                            <label class=\"block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1\">Cédula de Identidad *</label>\n                            <input type=\"text\" name=\"admin_dni\" required placeholder=\"V-12345678\"\n                                class=\"w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500\">\n                        </div>\n                        <div>\n                            <label class=\"block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1\">Teléfono</label>\n                            <input type=\"tel\" name=\"admin_phone\" placeholder=\"0414-1234567\"\n                                class=\"w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500\">\n                        </div>\n                    </div>\n\n                    <!-- Step 3: Review -->\n                    <div id=\"step-3\" class=\"hidden\">\n                        <div class=\"bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded-lg space-y-3\">\n                            <h4 class=\"font-semibold text-gray-900\">Datos del Condominio</h4>\n                            <div class=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-sm\">\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-500\">Nombre:</span>\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-900\" id=\"review-name\"></span>\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-500\">Slug:</span>\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-900\" id=\"review-slug\"></span>\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-500\">Dirección:</span>\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-900\" id=\"review-address\"></span>\n                            </div>\n                        </div>\n                        <div class=\"bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded-lg space-y-3 mt-4\">\n                            <h4 class=\"font-semibold text-gray-900\">Administrador Principal</h4>\n                            <div class=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-sm\">\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-500\">Nombre:</span>\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-900\" id=\"review-admin-name\"></span>\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-500\">Email:</span>\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-900\" id=\"review-admin-email\"></span>\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-500\">Cédula:</span>\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-900\" id=\"review-admin-dni\"></span>\n                            </div>\n                        </div>\n                        <div class=\"bg-amber-50 p-4 rounded-lg mt-4\">\n                            <p class=\"text-sm text-amber-700\">Al confirmar, se creará el condominio y se enviará un email de invitación al administrador para que complete su registro.</p>\n                        </div>\n                    </div>\n                </form>\n            </div>\n\n            <!-- Modal Footer -->\n            <div class=\"px-6 py-4 border-t border-gray-100 flex justify-between\">\n                <button type=\"button\" onclick=\"prevStep()\" id=\"btn-prev\" class=\"px-4 py-2 text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-800 hidden\">Anterior</button>\n                <div class=\"flex-1\"></div>\n                <button type=\"button\" onclick=\"nextStep()\" id=\"btn-next\" class=\"px-6 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition\">Siguiente</button>\n                <button type=\"button\" onclick=\"submitTenant()\" id=\"btn-submit\" class=\"px-6 py-2 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-700 transition hidden\">Crear Condominio</button>\n            </div>\n        </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- Toast Notifications -->\n    <div id=\"toast-container\" class=\"fixed bottom-4 right-4 z-50 space-y-2\"></div>\n\n    <script>\n        let currentStep = 1;\n        let tenantsData = [];\n        let token = localStorage.getItem('token');\n        let user = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('user') || '{}');\n\n        // Initialize\n        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {\n            if (!token || !user.isSuperAdmin) {\n                window.location.href = '/login';\n                return;\n            }\n\n            document.getElementById('adminName').textContent = user.firstName + ' ' + user.lastName;\n            document.getElementById('adminInitial').textContent = user.firstName?.[0] || 'A';\n\n            loadDashboard();\n        });\n\n        async function loadDashboard() {\n            await Promise.all([\n                loadStats(),\n                loadTenants(),\n                loadAuditLogs(),\n                loadActivity()\n            ]);\n        }\n\n        async function loadStats() {\n            try {\n                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/stats', {\n                    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }\n                });\n                if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load stats');\n                const data = await res.json();\n\n                document.getElementById('stat-tenants').textContent = data.tenants || 0;\n                document.getElementById('stat-tenants-active').textContent = data.tenants_active || data.tenants || 0;\n                document.getElementById('stat-users').textContent = data.users || 0;\n                document.getElementById('stat-owners').textContent = data.owners || 0;\n                document.getElementById('stat-properties').textContent = data.properties || 0;\n                document.getElementById('stat-revenue').textContent = '$' + (data.revenue || 0).toLocaleString();\n            } catch (error) {\n                console.error('Error loading stats:', error);\n                showToast('Error cargando estadísticas', 'error');\n            }\n        }\n\n        async function loadTenants() {\n            try {\n                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/tenants', {\n                    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }\n                });\n                if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load tenants');\n                tenantsData = await res.json();\n                renderTenants(tenantsData);\n            } catch (error) {\n                console.error('Error loading tenants:', error);\n                document.getElementById('tenants-table-body').innerHTML = `\n                    <tr><td colspan=\"5\" class=\"px-6 py-8 text-center text-red-500\">Error cargando condominios</td></tr>\n                `;\n            }\n        }\n\n        function renderTenants(tenants) {\n            const tbody = document.getElementById('tenants-table-body');\n            \n            if (tenants.length === 0) {\n                tbody.innerHTML = `\n                    <tr><td colspan=\"5\" class=\"px-6 py-8 text-center text-gray-500\">No hay condominios registrados</td></tr>\n                `;\n                return;\n            }\n\n            tbody.innerHTML = tenants.map(t => `\n                <tr class=\"hover:bg-gray-50 transition\">\n                    <td class=\"px-6 py-4\">\n                        <div class=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n                            <div class=\"w-10 h-10 bg-blue-100 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center\">\n                                <span class=\"text-blue-600 font-bold\">${t.name[0]}</span>\n                            </div>\n                            <div>\n                                <div class=\"font-medium text-gray-900\">${t.name}</div>\n                                <div class=\"text-sm text-gray-500\">${t.slug}</div>\n                            </div>\n                        </div>\n                    </td>\n                    <td class=\"px-6 py-4\">\n                        <div class=\"text-sm\">\n                            <div class=\"text-gray-900\">${t.property_count || 0} unidades</div>\n                            <div class=\"text-gray-500\">${t.user_count || 0} usuarios</div>\n                        </div>\n                    </td>\n                    <td class=\"px-6 py-4\">\n                        <span class=\"px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium ${t.active ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600'}\">\n                            ${t.active ? 'Activo' : 'Inactivo'}\n                        </span>\n                    </td>\n                    <td class=\"px-6 py-4 text-sm text-gray-500\">\n                        ${new Date(t.created_at).toLocaleDateString('es-VE')}\n                    </td>\n                    <td class=\"px-6 py-4 text-right\">\n                        <button onclick=\"viewTenant('${t.id}')\" class=\"text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 text-sm font-medium\">Ver</button>\n                    </td>\n                </tr>\n            `).join('');\n        }\n\n        function filterTenants() {\n            const search = document.getElementById('tenant-search').value.toLowerCase();\n            const filter = document.getElementById('tenant-filter').value;\n\n            let filtered = tenantsData;\n\n            if (search) {\n                filtered = filtered.filter(t => \n                    t.name.toLowerCase().includes(search) || \n                    t.slug.toLowerCase().includes(search)\n                );\n            }\n\n            if (filter === 'active') {\n                filtered = filtered.filter(t => t.active);\n            } else if (filter === 'inactive') {\n                filtered = filtered.filter(t => !t.active);\n            }\n\n            renderTenants(filtered);\n        }\n\n        async function loadActivity() {\n            try {\n                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/activity?limit=5', {\n                    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }\n                });\n                if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load activity');\n                const data = await res.json();\n\n                const container = document.getElementById('activity-list');\n                if (data.length === 0) {\n                    container.innerHTML = '<div class=\"text-center text-gray-500 py-4\">Sin actividad reciente</div>';\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                container.innerHTML = data.map(item => `\n                    <div class=\"flex items-start gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-50 transition\">\n                        <div class=\"w-8 h-8 rounded-full ${getActivityColor(item.action)} flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0\">\n                            ${getActivityIcon(item.action)}\n                        </div>\n                        <div class=\"flex-1 min-w-0\">\n                            <p class=\"text-sm text-gray-900\">${item.description}</p>\n                            <p class=\"text-xs text-gray-500\">${item.tenant_name || 'Sistema'} • ${timeAgo(item.created_at)}</p>\n                        </div>\n                    </div>\n                `).join('');\n            } catch (error) {\n                console.error('Error loading activity:', error);\n            }\n        }\n\n        async function loadAuditLogs() {\n            try {\n                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/audit-logs?limit=20', {\n                    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }\n                });\n                if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load audit logs');\n                const data = await res.json();\n\n                const container = document.getElementById('audit-logs-container');\n                if (data.length === 0) {\n                    container.innerHTML = '<div class=\"text-center text-gray-500 py-4\">Sin logs de auditoría</div>';\n                    return;\n                }\n\n                container.innerHTML = data.map(log => `\n                    <div class=\"flex items-center gap-3 p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg\">\n                        <span class=\"text-xs font-mono text-gray-400\">${new Date(log.created_at).toLocaleTimeString('es-VE')}</span>\n                        <span class=\"px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-medium ${getActionBadgeClass(log.action)}\">${log.action}</span>\n                        <span class=\"text-sm text-gray-700 flex-1\">${log.description}</span>\n                        <span class=\"text-xs text-gray-500\">${log.user_name || 'Sistema'}</span>\n                    </div>\n                `).join('');\n            } catch (error) {\n                console.error('Error loading audit logs:', error);\n                document.getElementById('audit-logs-container').innerHTML = \n                    '<div class=\"text-center text-red-500 py-4\">Error cargando auditoría</div>';\n            }\n        }\n\n        function getActivityColor(action) {\n            const colors = {\n                'TENANT_CREATED': 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600',\n                'USER_LOGIN': 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-600',\n                'TENANT_SUSPENDED': 'bg-red-100 text-red-600',\n                'default': 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600'\n            };\n            return colors[action] || colors.default;\n        }\n\n        function getActivityIcon(action) {\n            const icons = {\n                'TENANT_CREATED': '<svg class=\"w-4 h-4\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\"><path stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" stroke-width=\"2\" d=\"M12 4v16m8-8H4\"/></svg>',\n                'USER_LOGIN': '<svg class=\"w-4 h-4\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" document.getElementById('step-3').classList.add('hidden');\n            \n            // Show current step\n            document.getElementById(`step-${currentStep}`).classList.remove('hidden');\n\n items-center justify-center font-semibold text-sm';\n                    if (line) line.className = 'w-16 h-1 bg-blue-600';\n                } else {\n                    indicator.className = 'w-8    }\n\n            // Update buttons\n            document.getElementById('btn-prev').classList.toggle('hidden', currentStep === 1);\n        window.location.href = '/';\n        }\n\n        function showToast(message, type = 'info') {\n            const container = document.getElementById('toast-container');\n            const  data: result.recordset\n            });\n        } catch (error) {\n            console.error('Get audit logs error:', error);\n            res.status(500).json({ error: 'Error al obtener logs de           console.error('Error logging audit:', error);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nmodule.exports = AdminController;\n"

{
  "path": "/Users/jmelendez/Documents/github_repos/condominio360/src/routes/admin.js",
  "content": "const express = require('express');\nconst router = express.Router();\nconst AdminController = require('../controllers/AdminController');\n\n// Middleware to ensure user is SuperAdmin\nconst { authenticate } = require('../middleware/auth');\n\nconst verifySuperAdmin = (req, res, next) => {\n    if (!req.user || !req.user.isSuperAdmin) {\n        return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Access denied. Super Admin only.' });\n    }\n    next();\n};\n\n// Apply auth middleware to all admin routes\nrouter.use(authenticate);\nrouter.use(verifySuperAdmin);\n\n// ==================== STATS & DASHBOARD ====================\nrouter.get('/stats', AdminController.getStats);\nrouter.get('/activity', AdminController.getActivity);\nrouter.get('/audit-logs', AdminController.getAuditLogs);\n\n// ==================== TENANTS (CONDOMINIOS) ====================\nrouter.get('/tenants', AdminController.getAllTenants);\nrouter.get('/tenants/:id', AdminController.getTenantById);\nrouter.post('/tenants', AdminController.createTenant);\nrouter.put('/tenants/:id', AdminController.updateTenant);\nrouter.post('/tenants/:id/toggle-status', AdminController.toggleTenantStatus);\n\n// ==================== ONBOARDING WIZARD ====================\nrouter.post('/onboarding', AdminController.onboardTenant);\n\nmodule.exports = router;\n"
}
```
