## ✅ Landing + Super Admin Panel - Completado

### Phase 1: Landing Page (Marketing Site)

**Nuevo landing profesional** (`src/views/landing.ejs`):

| Sección | Descripción |
|---------|-------------|
| **Navbar fijo** | Navegación sticky con menú móvil |
| **Hero mejorado** | Formulario de demo, social proof (stars + avatares), mockup de dashboard |
| **Social Proof** | Logos de condominios de ejemplo |
| **Features (6)** | Votaciones, Visitantes, NFC, Solicitudes, Facturación, Reportes |
| **How it Works** | 3 pasos visuales con conectores |
| **Pricing** | 3 planes (Básico $2, Profesional $1.5, Empresarial $1 por unidad) |
| **Testimonios** | 3 tarjetas con fotos y reseñas |
| **FAQ (5)** | Acordeón interactivo con preguntas frecuentes |
| **CTA Final** | Formulario de demo destacado |
| **Footer** | 4 columnas con links y redes sociales |

### Phase 2: Super Admin Panel

**Dashboard rediseñado** (`src/views/admin/dashboard.ejs`):

| Componente | Funcionalidad |
|------------|---------------|
| **Sidebar** | Navegación fija con logout |
| **KPI Cards (4)** | Tenants, Usuarios, Unidades, Ingresos (con datos reales) |
| **Actividad Reciente** | Timeline de eventos globales |
| **Tabla de Tenants** | Búsqueda, filtros, stats por condominio |
| **Wizard Onboarding** | 3 pasos: Datos del condo → Admin → Review |
| **Auditoría Global** | Logs de actividad del sistema |

**APIs nuevas/mejoradas** (`/api/admin/*`):

```
GET  /api/admin/stats          → KPIs enriquecidos
GET  /api/admin/activity       → Actividad reciente
GET  /api/admin/audit-logs     → Auditoría detallada
GET  /api/admin/tenants        → Listado con stats
GET  /api/admin/tenants/:id    → Detalle completo
POST /api/admin/tenants        → Crear básico
PUT  /api/admin/tenants/:id    → Actualizar
POST /api/admin/tenants/:id/toggle-status
POST /api/admin/onboarding     → Wizard completo (tenant + admin)
```

### Estado del Sistema

```
📊 Total de tablas: 24
✅ 12 migraciones ejecutadas
✅ App carga sin errores
✅ Todas las rutas funcionan
```

### Flujo de Onboarding

1. Super Admin accede a `/admin`
2. Clic en "Nuevo Condominio"
3. Wizard de 3 pasos:
   - Paso 1: Nombre, slug, dirección
   - Paso 2: Datos del admin principal (nombre, email, cédula)
   - Paso 3: Review y confirmación
4. Se crea: Tenant + User + TenantAdmin + Invitación por email
5. Admin recibe email para completar registro

¿Listos para continuar con los siguientes capítulos? ¿O necesitas ajustes en el landing o dashboard?

     1	// Auth Logic
     2	const API_URL = '/api/auth';
     3	
     4	// Login Handler
     5	const loginForm = document.getElementById('loginForm');
     6	if (loginForm) {
     7	    loginForm.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
     8	        e.preventDefault();
     9	        const email = document.getElementById('email').value;
    10	        const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
    11	        const errorMessage = document.getElementById('errorMessage');
    12	
    13	        try {
    14	            const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/login`, {
    15	                method: 'POST',
    16	                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    17	                body: JSON.stringify({ email, password })
    18	            });
    19	
    20	            const data = await response.json();
    21	
    22	            if (response.ok) {
    23	                // 1. Super Admin Check
    24	                if (data.user && data.user.isSuperAdmin) {
    25	                    localStorage.setItem('token', data.token); // Global token
    26	                    localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(data.user));
    27	                    window.location.href = '/admin';
    28	                    return;
    29	                }
    30	
    31	                // 2. Tenant Selection needed
    32	                localStorage.setItem('tempToken', data.token); // Use Global Token as Temp
    33	                localStorage.setItem('availableTenants', JSON.stringify(data.availableTenants || []));
    34	
    35	                if (data.availableTenants && data.availableTenants.length === 1) {
    36	                    // Auto select if only one tenant
    37	                    selectTenant(data.availableTenants[0].id);
    38	                } else {
    39	                    window.location.href = '/select-tenant';
    40	                }
    41	            } else {
    42	                errorMessage.textContent = data.error || 'Error al iniciar sesión';
    43	                errorMessage.classList.remove('hidden');
    44	            }
    45	        } catch (error) {
    46	            console.error('Login error:', error);
    47	            errorMessage.textContent = 'Error de conexión';
    48	            errorMessage.classList.remove('hidden');
    49	        }
    50	    });
    51	}
    52	
    53	// Load Tenants for Selection
    54	async function loadTenantsForSelection() {
    55	    const tenantList = document.getElementById('tenantList');
    56	    if (!tenantList) return;
    57	
    58	    const tenants = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('availableTenants') || '[]');
    59	    const tempToken = localStorage.getItem('tempToken');
    60	
    61	    if (!tempToken || tenants.length === 0) {
    62	        window.location.href = '/login';
    63	        return;
    64	    }
    65	
    66	    tenantList.innerHTML = '';
    67	
    68	    tenants.forEach(tenant => {
    69	        const button = document.createElement('button');
    70	        button.className = 'w-full flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-4 border border-gray-300 shadow-sm text-sm font-medium rounded-md text-gray-700 bg-white hover:bg-gray-50 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-blue-500 transition';
    71	        button.innerHTML = `
    72	            <div class="flex flex-col items-start">
    73	                <span class="text-lg font-bold text-gray-900">${tenant.name}</span>
    74	                <span class="text-xs text-gray-500">ID: ${tenant.slug}</span>
    75	            </div>
    76	            <svg class="h-5 w-5 text-gray-400" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor">
    77	                <path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M7.293 14.707a1 1 0 010-1.414L10.586 10 7.293 6.707a1 1 0 011.414-1.414l4 4a1 1 0 010 1.414l-4 4a1 1 0 01-1.414 0z" clip-rule="evenodd" />
    78	            </svg>
    79	        `;
    80	
    81	        button.onclick = () => selectTenant(tenant.id);
    82	        tenantList.appendChild(button);
    83	    });
    84	}
    85	
    86	async function selectTenant(tenantId) {
    87	    const tempToken = localStorage.getItem('tempToken');
    88	    try {
    89	        const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/select-tenant`, {
    90	            method: 'POST',
    91	            headers: {
    92	                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    93	                'Authorization': `Bearer ${tempToken}`
    94	            },
    95	            body: JSON.stringify({ tenantId })
    96	        });
    97	
    98	        const data = await response.json();

   102	            // Note: AuthController.selectTenant currently doesn't return user object,

   104	            // checking if data.user exists (it was missing from controller before, assuming it might be added or using old one)

   106	
   107	            // Clear temp data
   108	            localStorage.removeItem('tempToken');
   109	            localStorage.removeItem('availableTenants');
   110	
   111	            if (data.role === 'ADMIN') {
   112	                window.location.href = '/tenant-admin';
   113	            } else {
   114	                window.location.href = '/dashboard';
   115	            }
   116	        } else {
   117	            alert('Error al seleccionar condominio: ' + (data.error || 'Unknown'));
   118	            window.location.href = '/login';
   119	        }
   120	    } catch (error) {
   121	        console.error('Select tenant error:', error);
   122	        alert('Error de conexión');
   123	    }
   124	}

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

I see the issue! In the `loginSuperAdmin` function, the user object returned doesn't include `isSuperAdmin: true`. Let me look at the return statement:

```javascript
return {
    token,
    user: {
        id: user.id,
        email: user.email,
        firstName: user.first_name,
        lastName: user.last_name,
        type: 'SUPERADMIN'
    }
};
```

The `isSuperAdmin` flag is missing from the user object! That's why the client-side code in auth.js can't detect it and redirects to the tenant selection instead. The fix is to add `isSuperAdmin: true` to the user object.

Let me fix this issue.

</details>

¡Encontré el problema! El `loginSuperAdmin` no incluye `isSuperAdmin: true` en el objeto user que retorna. Lo arreglo:

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

I see the issue. The unified login tries in this order:
1. If type === 'TENANT_ADMIN' → loginTenantAdmin
2. If type === 'SUPERADMIN' → loginSuperAdmin  
3. Otherwise: try loginOwner, then loginTenantAdmin

The problem is that if someone tries to login as superadmin without specifying type, it will try loginOwner first, which might fail with "Credenciales inválidas" and then try loginTenantAdmin which will also fail. The superadmin login is only tried if explicitly specified with type === 'SUPERADMIN'.

Let me update the login flow to also try superadmin in the auto-detection flow. But actually, looking at the client-side code, when a user logs in from the regular login page, they don't specify a type, so the superadmin would need to be detected automatically.

Let me modify the login flow to try superadmin as well when auto-detecting.

</details>

var(--md-sys-color-surface);\n            padding: 0 4px;\n            color: var(--md-sys-color-on-surface-variant);\n            font-size: 16px;\n            transition: all 0.2s;\n            pointer-events: none;\n        }\n        \n        .md-text-field input:focus + label,\n        .md-text-field input:not(:placeholder-shown) + label,\n        .md-text-field textarea:focus + label,\n        .md-text-field textarea:not(:placeholder-shown) + label {\n            top: 0;\n            font-size: 12px;\n            color: var(--md-sys-color-primary);\n        }\n        \n        /* Buttons Material */\n        .md-button {\n            display: inline-flex;\n            align-items: center;\n            justify-content: center;\n            gap: 8px;\n            padding: 10px 24px;\n            border-radius: var(--md-radius-extra-large);\n            font-size: 14px;\n            font-weight: 600;\n            letter-spacing: 0.5px;\n            text-transform: uppercase;\n            transition: all 0.2s;\n            cursor: pointer;\n            border: none;\n        }\n        \n        .md-button-filled {\n            background: var(--md-sys-color-primary);\n            color: var(--md-sys-color-on-primary);\n        }\n        \n        .md-button-filled:hover {\n            box-shadow: var(--md-elevation-1);\n        }\n        \n        .md-button-tonal {\n            background: var(--md-sys-color-secondary-container);\n            color: var(--md-sys-color-on-secondary-container);\n        }\n        \n        .md-button-outlined {\n            background: transparent;\n            color: var(--md-sys-color-primary);\n            border: 1px solid var(--md-sys-color-outline);\n        }\n        \n        .md-button-text {\n            background: transparent;\n            color: var(--md-sys-color-primary);\n            padding: 10px 12px;\n        }\n        \n        /* Progress Indicator */\n        .md-linear-progress {\n            height: 4px;\n            background: var(--md-sys-color-surface-variant);\n            border-radius: 2px;\n            overflow: hidden;\n        }\n        \n        .md-linear-progress-bar {\n            height: 100%;\n            background: var(--md-sys-color-primary);\n            transition: width 0.3s;\n        }\n        \n        /* Segmented Button */\n        .md-segmented-button {\n            display: flex;\n            background: var(--md-sys-color-surface-variant);\n            border-radius: var(--md-radius-extra-large);\n            padding: 4px;\n            gap: 4px;\n        }\n        \n        .md-segment {\n            flex: 1;\n            padding: 8px 16px;\n            border-radius: var(--md-radius-extra-large);\n            text-align: center;\n            font-size: 14px;\n            font-weight: 500;\n            cursor: pointer;\n            transition: all 0.2s;\n            color: var(--md-sys-color-on-surface);\n        }\n        \n        .md-segment.active {\n            background: var(--md-sys-color-secondary-container);\n            color: var(--md-sys-color-on-secondary-container);\n        }\n        \n        /* Snackbar */\n        .md-snackbar {\n            position: fixed;\n            bottom: 24px;\n            left: 50%;\n            transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(100px);\n            background: var(--md-sys-color-on-surface);\n            color: var(--md-sys-color-surface);\n            padding: 14px 24px;\n            border-radius: var(--md-radius-small);\n            font-size: 14px;\n            box-shadow: var(--md-elevation-3);\n            z-index: 200;\n            opacity: 0;\n            transition: all 0.3s;\n            display: flex;\n            align-items: center;\n            gap: 24px;\n        }\n        \n        .md-snackbar.active {\n            transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(0);\n            opacity: 1;\n        }\n        \n        /* Animations */\n        @keyframes slideIn {\n            from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(20px); }\n            to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }\n        }\n        \n        .animate-slide-in {\n            animation: slideIn 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);\n        }\n        \n        /* Table Material */\n        .md-table-container {\n            overflow-x: auto;\n            border-radius: var(--md-radius-large);\n        }\n        \n        .md-table {\n            width: 100%;\n            border-collapse: collapse;\n        }\n        \n        .md-table th {\n            text-align: left;\n            padding: 16px;\n            font-size: 12px;\n            font-weight: 600;\n            letter-spacing: 0.5px;\n            text-transform: uppercase;\n            color: var(--md-sys-color-on-surface-variant);\n            border-bottom: 1px solid var(--md-sys-color-surface-variant);\n        }\n        \n        .md-table td {\n            padding: 16px;\n            border-bottom: 1px solid var(--md-sys-color-surface-variant);\n            color: var(--md-sys-color-on-surface);\n        }\n        \n        .md-table tr:hover td {\n            background: rgba(0, 100, 148, 0.04);\n        }\n        \n        /* Chips */\n        .md-chip {\n            display: inline-flex;\n            align-items: center;\n            gap: 8px;\n            padding: 8px 16px;\n            border-radius: var(--md-radius-small);\n            font-size: 14px;\n            font-weight: 500;\n        }\n        \n        .md-chip-filled {\n            background: var(--md-sys-color-primary-container);\n            color: var(--md-sys-color-on-primary-container);\n        }\n        \n        /* Stats Cards */\n        .stat-card {\n            padding: 24px;\n            border-radius: var(--md-radius-large);\n            position: relative;\n            overflow: hidden;\n        }\n        \n        .stat-card::before {\n            content: '';\n            position: absolute;\n            top: 0;\n            left: 0;\n            right: 0;\n            height: 4px;\n        }\n        \n        .stat-card.primary::before { background: var(--md-sys-color-primary); }\n        .stat-card.secondary::before { background: var(--md-sys-color-secondary); }\n        .stat-card.tertiary::before { background: #6b5b4f; }\n        .stat-card.error::before { background: var(--md-sys-color-error); }\n        \n        /* App Top Bar */\n        .app-top-bar {\n            background: var(--md-sys-color-surface);\n            height: 64px;\n            display: flex;\n            align-items: center;\n            padding: 0 16px;\n            gap: 16px;\n            box-shadow: var(--md-elevation-1);\n        }\n    </style>\n</head>\n<body class=\"bg-gray-50\">\n    <div class=\"min-h-screen flex\">\n        <!-- Navigation Rail / Sidebar -->\n        <aside id=\"sidebar\" class=\"nav-rail expanded fixed h-full z-40 flex flex-col\">\n            <!-- Logo Section -->\n            <div class=\"p-4 flex items-center gap-4 h-16\">\n                <button id=\"menu-toggle\" class=\"w-12 h-12 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full hover:bg-gray-100 transition\">\n                    <span class=\"material-symbols-rounded text-gray-700\">menu</span>\n                </button>\n                <div class=\"flex items-center gap-3 overflow-hidden\">\n                    <div class=\"w-10 h-10 bg-[var(--md-sys-color-primary)] rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0\">\n                        <span class=\"text-white font-bold text-xl\">C</span>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"whitespace-nowrap\">\n                        <h1 class=\"font-semibold text-gray-900 text-lg leading-tight\">Condominio360</h1>\n                        <p class=\"text-xs text-gray-500\">Super Admin</p>\n                    </div>\n                </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <!-- Divider -->\n            <div class=\"h-px bg-gray-200 mx-4\"></div>\n\n            <!-- Navigation Items -->\n            <nav class=\"flex-1 py-4 overflow-y-auto\">\n                <div class=\"nav-item active\" onclick=\"showSection('dashboard')\">\n                    <span class=\"icon material-symbols-rounded\">space_dashboard</span>\n                    <span class=\"label\">Dashboard</span>\n                </div>\n                <div material-symbols-rounded\">group</span>\n                    <span class=\"label\">Usuarios</span>\n                </div>\n                <div class=\"nav-item\" onclick=\"showSection('audit')\">\n class=\"nav-item\" onclick=\"showSection('settings')\">\n                    <span class=\"icon material-symbols-rounded\">settings</span>\n                    <span class=\"label\">Configuración</span>\n                </div>\n            </nav>\n\n            <!-- Bottom Actions -->\n            <div class=\"p-4 border-t border-gray-200\">\n                <div class=\"nav-item text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50\" onclick=\"logout()\">\n                    <span class=\"icon material-symbols-rounded\">logout</span>\n                    <span class=\"label\">Cerrar sesión</span>\n                </div>\n            </div>\n        </aside>\n\n        <!-- Main Content -->\n        <main id=\"main-content\" class=\"flex-1 ml-[280px] transition-all duration-300\">\n            <!-- Top App Bar -->\n            <header class=\"app-top-bar sticky top-0 z-30\">\n                <h2 id=\"page-title\" class=\"text-xl font-medium text-gray-900 flex-1\">Dashboard</h2>\n                <div class=\"flex items-center gap-4\">\n                    <span id=\"admin-name\" class=\"text-sm text-gray-600 hidden sm:block\">Admin</span>\n                    <div class=\"w-10 h-10 bg-[var(--md-sys-color-primary-container)] rounded-full flex items-center justify-center\">\n                        <span id=\"admin-initial\" class=\"text-[var(--md-sys-color-on-primary-container)] font-medium\">A</span>\n                    </div>\n                </div>\n            </header>\n\n            <!-- Content Area -->\n            <div class=\"p-6 max-w-7xl mx-auto\">\n                <!-- Dashboard Section -->\n                <section id=\"section-dashboard\" class=\"space-y-6\">\n                    <!-- Stats Grid -->\n                    <div class=\"grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6\">\n                        <!-- Stat Card 1 -->\n                        <div class=\"stat-card primary md-card animate-slide-in\" style=\"animation-delay: 0ms;\">\n                            <div class=\"flex items-start justify-between\">\n                                <div>\n                                    <p class=\"text-sm text-gray-500 font-medium\">Condominios</p>\n                                    <h3 id=\"stat-tenants\" class=\"text-4xl font-light text-gray-900 mt-2\">0</h3>\n                                    <p class=\"text-sm text-emerald-600 mt-1 flex items-center gap-1\">\n                                        <span class=\"material-symbols-rounded text-sm\">trending_up</span>\n                                        <span id=\"stat-tenants-active\">0</span> activos\n                                    </p>\n                                </div>\n                                <div class=\"w-12 h-12 bg-blue-100 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center\">\n                                    <span class=\"material-symbols-rounded text-blue-600 text-2xl\">apartment</span>\n                                </div>\n                            </div>\n                        </div>\n\n                        <!-- Stat Card 2 -->\n                        <div class=\"stat-card secondary md-card animate-slide-in\" style=\"animation-delay: 50ms;\">\n                            <div class=\"flex items-start justify-between\">\n                                <div>\n                                    <p class=\"text-sm text-gray-500 font-medium\">Usuarios Totales</p>\n                                    <h3 id=\"stat-users\" class=\"text-4xl font-light text-gray-900 mt-2\">0</h3>\n                                    <p class=\"text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1\">\n                                        <span id=\"stat-owners\">0</span> propietarios\n                                    </p>\n                                </div>\n                                <div class=\"w-12 h-12 bg-emerald-100 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center\">\n                                    <span class=\"material-symbols-rounded text-emerald-600 text-2xl\">group</span>\n                                </div>\n                            </div>\n                        </div>\n\n                        <!-- Stat Card 3 -->\n                        <div class=\"stat-card tertiary md-card animate-slide-in\" style=\"animation-delay: 100ms;\">\n                            <div class=\"flex items-start justify-between\">\n                                <div>\n                                    <p class=\"text-sm text-gray-500 font-medium\">Unidades</p>\n                                    <h3 id=\"stat-properties\" class=\"text-4xl font-light text-gray-900 mt-2\">0</h3>\n                                    <p class=\"text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1\">Inmobiliarias</p>\n                                </div>\n                                <div class=\"w-12 h-12 bg-amber-100 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center\">\n                                    <span class=\"material-symbols-rounded text-amber-600 text-2xl\">home_work</span>\n                                </div>\n                            </div>\n                        </div>\n\n                        <!-- Stat Card 4 -->\n                        <div class=\"stat-card error md-card animate-slide-in\" style=\"animation-delay:                                oninput=\"filterTenants()\">\n                        </div>\n                        <div class=\"md-segmented-button w-full sm:w-auto\">\n                            <div class=\"md-segment active\" onclick=\"setFilter('all', this)\">Todos</div>\n                            <div class=\"md-segment\" onclick=\"setFilter('active', this)\">Activos</div>\n                            <div class=\"md-segment\" onclick=\"setFilter('inactive', this)\">Inactivos</div>\n                        </div>\n                    </div>\n\n                    <!-- Tenants Table -->\n                    <div class=\"md-card overflow-hidden\">\n                        <div class=\"md-table-container\">\n                            <table class=\"md-table\">\n <th></th>\n                                    </tr>\n                                </thead>\n                                <tbody id=\"tenants-table-body\">\n class=\"md-modal\">\n        <!-- Modal Header -->\n        <div class=\"px-6 py-4 border-b border-gray-100 flex items-center justify-between bg-gray-50/50\">\n            <div>\n                <h3 class=\"text-xl font-medium text-gray-900\">Nuevo Condominio</h3>\n                <p class=\"text-sm text-gray-500\">Completa la información para crear un nuevo conjunto residencial</p>\n            </div>\n            <button onclick=\"closeNewTenantModal()\" class=\"w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full hover:bg-gray-100 transition\">\n                <span border-gray-100\">\n            <div class=\"flex items-center gap-4\">\n                <div id=\"step-indicator-1\" class=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                    <div class=\"w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[var(--md-sys-color-primary)] text-white flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-medium\">1</div>\n                    <span class=\"text-sm font-medium text-gray-900 hidden sm:inline\">Condominio</span>\n                </div>\n                <div class=\"flex-1 h-0.5 bg-gray-200 max-w-[60px]\"></div>\n                <div id=\"step-indicator-2\" class=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                    <div class=\"w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-gray-200 text-gray-500 flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-medium\">2</div>\n                    <span class=\"text-sm font-medium text-gray-500 hidden sm:inline\">Administrador</span>\n                </div>\n                <div class=\"flex-1 h-0.5 bg-gray-200 max-w-[60px]\"></div>\n                <div id=\"step-indicator-3\" class=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                    <div class=\"w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-gray-200 text-gray-500 flex items-center justify-center text-sm md-linear-progress\">\n                <div id=\"progress-bar\" class=\"md-linear-progress-bar\" style=\"width: 33%\"></div>\n            </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <!-- Modal Body -->\n        <div class=\"p-6 overflow-y-auto max-h-[50vh]\">\n            <form id=\"new-tenant-form\">\n                <!-- Step 1: Tenant Info -->\n                <div id=\"step-1\" class=\"space-y-4\">\n                    <div class=\"md-text-field\">\n                        <input type=\"text\" id=\"tenant-name\" name=\"name\" required placeholder=\" \">\n                        <label for=\"tenant-name\">Nombre del Condominio *</label>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"md-text-field\">\n                        <input type=\"text\" id=\"tenant-slug\" name=\"slug\" required placeholder=\" \" \n                            pattern=\"[a-z0-9-]+\" oninput=\"this.value = this.value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/g, '')\">\n                        <label for=\"tenant-slug\">Slug (identificador único) *</label>\n                        <p class=\"text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1 ml-2\">Solo minúsculas, números y guiones</p>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"md-text-field\">\n                        <textarea id=\"tenant-address\" name=\"address\" rows=\"3\" placeholder=\" \"></textarea>\n                        <label for=\"tenant-address\">Dirección</label>\n                    </div>\n                </div>\n\n                <!-- Step 2: Admin Info -->\n                <div id=\"step-2\" class=\"space-y-4 hidden\">\n                    <div class=\"bg-blue-50 p-4 rounded-xl mb-4\">\n                        <p class=\"text-sm text-blue-700 flex items-center gap-2\">\n                            <span class=\"material-symbols-rounded text-blue-600\">info</span>\n                            Crea el primer administrador de junta para este condominio\n                        </p>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-4\">\n                        <div class=\"md-text-field\">\n                            <input type=\"text\" id=\"admin-first-name\" name=\"first_name\" required placeholder=\" \">\n                            <label for=\"admin-first-name\">Nombre *</label>\n                        </div>\n                        <div class=\"md-text-field\">\n                            <input type=\"text\" id=\"admin-last-name\" name=\"last_name\" required placeholder=\" \">\n                            <label for=\"admin-last-name\">Apellido *</label>\n                        </div>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"md-text-field\">\n                        <input type=\"email\" id=\"admin-email\" name=\"email\" required placeholder=\" \">\n                        <label for=\"admin-email\">Email *</label>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"md-text-field\">\n                        <input type=\"text\" id=\"admin-dni\" name=\"dni\" required placeholder=\" \" \n                            oninput=\"formatDNI(this)\">\n                        <label for=\"admin-dni\">Cédula de Identidad *</label>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"md-text-field\">\n                        <input type=\"tel\" id=\"admin-phone\" name=\"phone\" placeholder=\" \">\n                        <label for=\"admin-phone\">Teléfono</label>\n                    </div>\n                </div>\n\n                <!-- Step 3: Review -->\n                <div id=\"step-3\" class=\"hidden space-y-4\">\n                    <div class=\"bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded-xl\">\n                        <h4 class=\"font-medium text-gray-900 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2\">\n                            <span class=\"material-symbols-rounded text-gray-500\">apartment</span>\n                            Datos del Condominio\n                        </h4>\n                        <div class=\"space-y-2 text-sm\">\n                            <div class=\"flex justify-between\">\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-500\">Nombre:</span>\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-900 font-medium\" id=\"review-name\"></span>\n                            </div>\n                            <div class=\"flex justify-between\">\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-500\">Slug:</span>\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-900 font-medium\" id=\"review-slug\"></span>\n                            </div>\n                            <div class=\"flex justify-between\">\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-500\">Dirección:</span>\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-900\" id=\"review-address\"></span>\n                            </div>\n                        </div>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded-xl\">\n                        <h4 class=\"font-medium text-gray-900 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2\">\n                            <span class=\"material-symbols-rounded text-gray-500\">person</span>\n                            Administrador Principal\n                        </h4>\n                        <div class=\"space-y-2 text-sm\">\n                            <div class=\"flex justify-between\">\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-500\">Nombre:</span>\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-900 font-medium\" id=\"review-admin-name\"></span>\n                            </div>\n                            <div class=\"flex justify-between\">\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-500\">Email:</span>\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-900\" id=\"review-admin-email\"></span>\n                            </div>\n                            <div class=\"flex justify-between\">\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-500\">Cédula:</span>\n                                <span class=\"text-gray-900\" id=\"review-admin-dni\"></span>\n                            </div>\n                        </div>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"bg-amber-50 p-4 rounded-xl flex items-start gap-3\">\n                        <span class=\"material-symbols-rounded text-amber-600\">mail</span>\n                        <p class=\"text-sm text-amber-700\">Al confirmar, se enviará un email de invitación al administrador para que complete su registro.</p>\n                    </div>\n                </div>\n            </form>\n        </div>\n\n        <!-- Modal Footer -->\n        <div class=\"px-6 py-4 border-t border-gray-100 flex justify-between bg-gray-50/50\">\n            <button type=\"button\" id=\"btn-prev\" class=\"md-button md-button-text hidden\" onclick=\"prevStep()\">\n                <span class=\"material-symbols-rounded\">arrow_back</span>\n                Anterior\n            </button>\n            <div class=\"flex-1\"></div>\n            <button type=\"button\" id=\"btn-next\" class=\"md-button md-button-filled\" onclick=\"nextStep()\">\n                Siguiente\n                <span class=\"material-symbols-rounded\">arrow_forward</span>\n            </button>\n            <button type=\"button\" id=\"btn-submit\" class=\"md-button md-button-filled hidden\" onclick=\"submitTenant()\">\n                <span class=\"material-symbols-rounded\">check</span>\n                Crear Condominio\n            </button>\n        </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- Snackbar -->\n    <div id=\"snackbar\" class=\"md-snackbar\">\n        <span id=\"snackbar-message\">Mensaje</span>\n        <button onclick=\"hideSnackbar()\" class=\"text-[var(--md-sys-color-primary)] font-medium uppercase text-sm\">Cerrar</button>\n    </div>\n\n    <script>\n        // Global State\n        let currentStep = 1;\n        let tenantsData = [];\n        let currentFilter = 'all';\n        let token = localStorage.getItem('token');\n        let user = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('user') || '{}');\n\n        // Initialize\n        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {\n            if (!token || !user.isSuperAdmin) {\n                window.location.href = '/login';\n                return;\n            }\n\n            // Set user info\n            document.getElementById('admin-name').textContent = (user.firstName || '') + ' ' + (user.lastName || '');\n            document.getElementById('admin-initial').textContent = (user.firstName || 'A')[0];\n\n            // Load data\n            loadDashboard();\n\n            // Setup sidebar toggle\n            document.getElementById('menu-toggle').addEventListener('click', toggleSidebar);\n        });\n\n        // Sidebar Toggle\n        function toggleSidebar() {\n            const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');\n            const mainContent = document.getElementById('main-content');\n            const icon = document.querySelector('#menu-toggle .material-symbols-rounded');\n            \n            if (sidebar.classList.contains('expanded')) {\n                sidebar.classList.remove('expanded');\n                sidebar.classList.add('collapsed');\n                mainContent.style.marginLeft = '80px';\n                icon.textContent = 'menu_open';\n            } else {\n                sidebar.classList.remove('collapsed');\n                sidebar.classList.add('expanded');\n                mainContent.style.marginLeft = '280px';\n                icon.textContent = 'menu';\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Section Navigation\n        function showSection(sectionName) {\n            // Hide all sections\n            document.querySelectorAll('main > div > section').forEach(s => s.classList.add('hidden'));\n            \n            // Show selected\n            document.getElementById(`section-${sectionName}`).classList.remove('hidden');\n            \n            // Update nav items\n            document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(item => item.classList.remove('active'));\n            event.currentTarget.classList.add('active');\n            \n            // Update page title\n            const titles = {\n                'dashboard': 'Dashboard',\n                'tenants': 'Condominios',\n                'users': 'Usuarios',\n                'audit': 'Auditoría',\n                'settings': 'Configuración'\n            };\n            document.getElementById('page-title').textContent = titles[sectionName];\n\n            // Load section data if needed\n            if (sectionName === 'tenants' && tenantsData.length === 0) {\n                loadTenants();\n            } else if (sectionName === 'audit') {\n                loadAuditLogs();\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Data Loading\n        async function loadDashboard() {\n            await Promise.all([\n                loadStats(),\n                loadActivity()\n            ]);\n        }\n\n        async function loadStats() {\n            try {\n                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/stats', {\n                    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }\n                });\n                const { data } = await res.json();\n\n                animateNumber('stat-tenants', data.tenants || 0);\n                animateNumber('stat-users', data.users || 0);\n                animateNumber('stat-properties', data.properties || 0);\n                document.getElementById('stat-tenants-active').textContent = data.tenants_active || 0;\n                document.getElementById('stat-owners').textContent = data.owners || 0;\n                document.getElementById('stat-revenue').textContent = '$' + (data.revenue || 0).toLocaleString();\n            } catch (error) {\n                showSnackbar('Error cargando estadísticas', 'error');\n            }\n        }\n\n        async function loadActivity() {\n            try {\n                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/activity?limit=5', {\n                    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }\n                });\n                const { data } = await res.json();\n                renderActivity(data);\n            } catch (error) {\n                console.error('Error loading activity:', error);\n            }\n        }\n\n        async function loadTenants() {\n            try {\n                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/tenants', {\n                    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }\n                });\n                const { data } = await res.json();\n                tenantsData = data;\n                renderTenants(data);\n            } catch (error) {\n                showSnackbar('Error cargando condominios', 'error');\n            }\n        }\n\n        async function loadAuditLogs() {\n            try {\n                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/audit-logs?limit=50', {\n                    headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }\n                });\n                const { data } = await res.json();\n                renderAuditLogs(data);\n            } catch (error) {\n                showSnackbar('Error cargando auditoría', 'error');\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Rendering\n        function renderActivity(items) {\n            const container = document.getElementById('activity-list');\n            if (!items || items.length === 0) {\n                container.innerHTML = '<p class=\"text-gray-500 text-center py-4\">Sin actividad reciente</p>';\n                return;\n            }\n\n            container.innerHTML = items.map(item => `\n                <div class=\"flex items-center gap-4 p-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-gray-50 transition\">\n                    <div class=\"w-10 h-10 rounded-full ${getActivityColor(item.action)} flex items-center justify-center\">\n                        <span class=\"material-symbols-rounded text-lg\">${getActivityIcon(item.action)}</span>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"flex-1 min-w-0\">\n                        <p class=\"text-gray-900 text-sm font-medium truncate\">${item.description}</p>\n                        <p class=\"text-gray-500 text-xs\">${item.tenant_name || 'Sistema'} • ${timeAgo(item.created_at)}</p>\n                    </div>\n                </div>\n            `).join('');\n        }\n\n        function renderTenants(tenants) {\n            const tbody = document.getElementById('tenants-table-body');\n            \n            if (tenants.length === 0) {\n                tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan=\"6\" class=\"text-center py-8 text-gray-500\">No hay condominios registrados</td></tr>';\n                return;\n            }\n\n            tbody.innerHTML = tenants.map(t => `\n                <tr>\n                    <td>\n                        <div class=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n                            <div class=\"w-10 h-10 bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-500 to-indigo-600 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-white font-bold\">\n                                ${t.name.charAt(0)}\n                            </div>\n                            <div>\n                                <div class=\"font-medium text-gray-900\">${t.name}</div>\n                                <div class=\"text-sm text-gray-500\">${t.slug}</div>\n                            </div>\n                        </div>\n                    </td>\n                    <td class=\"font-medium\">${t.property_count || 0}</td>\n                    <td class=\"font-medium\">${t.user_count || 0}</td>\n                    <td>\n                        <span class=\"md-chip ${t.active ? 'md-chip-filled' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600'}\">\n                            ${t.active ? 'Activo' : 'Inactivo'}\n                        </span>\n                    </td>\n                    <td class=\"text-gray-500\">${new Date(t.created_at).toLocaleDateString('es-VE')}</td>\n                    <td>\n                        <button class=\"w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full hover:bg-gray-100 transition\" onclick=\"viewTenant('${t.id}')\">\n                            <span class=\"material-symbols-rounded text-gray-500\">more_vert</span>\n                        </button>\n                    </td>\n                </tr>\n            `).join('');\n        }\n\n        function renderAuditLogs(logs) {\n            const container = document.getElementById('audit-logs-container');\n            if (!logs || logs.length === 0) {\n                container.innerHTML = '<p class=\"text-gray-500 text-center py-8\">Sin registros</p>';\n                return;\n            }\n\n            container.innerHTML = logs.map(log => `\n                <div class=\"flex items-center gap-4 p-4 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl\">\n                    <span class=\"text-xs font-mono text-gray-400\">${new Date(log.created_at).toLocaleTimeString('es-VE')}</span>\n                    <span class=\"px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium ${getActionBadgeClass(log.action)}\">${log.action}</span>\n                    <span class=\"text-sm text-gray-700 flex-1\">${log.description}</span>\n                    <span class=\"text-xs text-gray-500\">${log.user_name || 'Sistema'}</span>\n                </div>\n            `).join('');\n        }\n\n        // Filter Functions\n        function setFilter(filter, element) {\n            currentFilter = filter;\n            document.querySelectorAll('.md-segment').forEach(s => s.classList.remove('active'));\n            element.classList.add('active');\n            filterTenants();\n        }\n\n        function filterTenants() {\n            const search = document.getElementById('tenant-search').value.toLowerCase();\n            \n            let filtered = tenantsData.filter(t => \n                t.name.toLowerCase().includes(search) || \n                t.slug.toLowerCase().includes(search)\n            );\n\n            if (currentFilter === 'active') filtered = filtered.filter(t => t.active);\n            if (currentFilter === 'inactive') filtered = filtered.filter(t => !t.active);\n\n            renderTenants(filtered);\n        }\n\n        // Wizard Functions\n        function openNewTenantModal() {\n            document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.add('active');\n            document.getElementById('new-tenant-modal').classList.add('active');\n            resetWizard();\n        }\n\n        function closeNewTenantModal() {\n            document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('active');\n            document.getElementById('new-tenant-modal').classList.remove('active');\n        }\n\n        function resetWizard() {\n            currentStep = 1;\n            document.getElementById('new-tenant-form').reset();\n            updateWizardUI();\n        }\n\n        function updateWizardUI() {\n            // Hide all steps\n            document.getElementById('step-1').classList.add('hidden');\n            document.getElementById('step-2').classList.add('hidden');\n            document.getElementById('step-3').classList.add('hidden');\n            \n            // Show current\n            document.getElementById(`step-${currentStep}`).classList.remove('hidden');\n\n            // Update progress\n            const progress = (currentStep / 3) * 100;\n            document.getElementById('progress-bar').style.width = progress + '%';\n\n            // Update step indicators\n            for (let i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {\n                const indicator = document.getElementById(`step-indicator-${i}`).querySelector('div');\n                const label = document.getElementById(`step-indicator-${i}`).querySelector('span');\n                \n                if (i <= currentStep) {\n                    indicator.className = 'w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[var(--md-sys-color-primary)] text-white flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-medium';\n                    if (label) {\n                        label.classList.remove('text-gray-500');\n                        label.classList.add('text-gray-900');\n                    }\n                } else {\n                    indicator.className = 'w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-gray-200 text-gray-500 flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-medium';\n                    if (label) {\n                        label.classList.remove('text-gray-900');\n                        label.classList.add('text-gray-500');\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Update buttons\n            document.getElementById('btn-prev').classList.toggle('hidden', currentStep === 1);\n            document.getElementById('btn-next').classList.toggle('hidden', currentStep === 3);\n            document.getElementById('btn-submit').classList.toggle('hidden', currentStep !== 3);\n\n            if (currentStep === 3) updateReviewData();\n        }\n\n        function updateReviewData() {\n            document.getElementById('review-name').textContent = document.getElementById('tenant-name').value;\n            document.getElementById('review-slug').textContent = document.getElementById('tenant-slug').value;\n            document.getElementById('review-address').textContent = document.getElementById('tenant-address').value || 'No especificada';\n            document.getElementById('review-admin-name').textContent = \n                document.getElementById('admin-first-name').value + ' ' + document.getElementById('admin-last-name').value;\n            document.getElementById('review-admin-email').textContent = document.getElementById('admin-email').value;\n            document.getElementById('review-admin-dni').textContent = document.getElementById('admin-dni').value;\n        }\n\n        function nextStep() {\n            if (validateStep(currentStep)) {\n                if (currentStep < 3) {\n                    currentStep++;\n                    updateWizardUI();\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        function prevStep() {\n            if (currentStep > 1) {\n                currentStep--;\n                updateWizardUI();\n            }\n        }\n\n        function validateStep(step) {\n            const ids = step === 1 \n                ? ['tenant-name', 'tenant-slug'] \n                : ['admin-first-name', 'admin-last-name', 'admin-email', 'admin-dni'];\n            \n            for (const id of ids) {\n                const el = document.getElementById(id);\n                if (!el.value.trim()) {\n                    el.focus();\n                    el.parentElement.classList.add('animate-pulse');\n                    setTimeout(() => el.parentElement.classList.remove('animate-pulse'), 500);\n                    showSnackbar('Completa todos los campos requeridos');\n                    return false;\n                }\n            }\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        async function submitTenant() {\n            const data = {\n                name: document.getElementById('tenant-name').value,\n                slug: document.getElementById('tenant-slug').value,\n                address: document.getElementById('tenant-address').value,\n                admin: {\n                    first_name: document.getElementById('admin-first-name').value,\n                    last_name: document.getElementById('admin-last-name').value,\n                    email: document.getElementById('admin-email').value,\n                    dni: document.getElementById('admin-dni').value,\n                    phone: document.getElementById('admin-phone').value\n                }\n            };\n\n            try {\n                const res = await fetch('/api/admin/onboarding', {\n                    method: 'POST',\n                    headers: {\n                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n                        'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`\n                    },\n                    body: JSON.stringify(data)\n                });\n\n                if (!res.ok) {\n                    const err = await res.json();\n                    throw new Error(err.error || 'Error creando condominio');\n                }\n\n                closeNewTenantModal();\n                showSnackbar('Condominio creado exitosamente', 'success');\n                loadTenants();\n                loadStats();\n            } catch (error) {\n                showSnackbar(error.message, 'error');\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Helpers\n        function getActivityColor(action) {\n            const colors = {\n                'TENANT_CREATED': 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600',\n                'USER_LOGIN': 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-600',\n                'TENANT_SUSPENDED': 'bg-red-100 text-red-600'\n            };\n            return colors[action] || 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600';\n        }\n\n        function getActivityIcon(action) {\n            const icons = {\n                'TENANT_CREATED': 'add_circle',\n                'USER_LOGIN': 'login',\n                'TENANT_SUSPENDED': 'block'\n            };\n            return icons[action] || 'info';\n        }\n\n        function getActionBadgeClass(action) {\n            const classes = {\n                'CREATE': 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700',\n                'UPDATE': 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700',\n                'DELETE': 'bg-red-100 text-red-700'\n            };\n            return classes[action] || 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700';\n        }\n\n        function timeAgo(date) {\n            const seconds = Math.floor((new Date() - new Date(date)) / 1000);\n            if (seconds < 60) return 'ahora';\n            const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);\n            if (minutes < 60) return `hace ${minutes}m`;\n            const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);\n            if (hours < 24) return `hace ${hours}h`;\n            return `hace ${Math.floor(hours / 24)}d`;\n        }\n\n        function animateNumber(id, target) {\n            const el = document.getElementById(id);\n            const duration = 1000;\n            const start = 0;\n            const increment = target / (duration / 16);\n            let current = start;\n            \n            const timer = setInterval(() => {\n                current += increment;\n                if (current >= target) {\n                    el.textContent = target.toLocaleString();\n                    clearInterval(timer);\n                } else {\n                    el.textContent = Math.floor(current).toLocaleString();\n                }\n            }, 16);\n        }\n\n        function formatDNI(input) {\n            let value = input.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');\n            if (value.length > 0 && !value.startsWith('V') && !value.startsWith('E')) {\n                value = 'V-' + value;\n            } else if (value.length > 0) {\n                value = value.substring(0, 1).toUpperCase() + '-' + value.substring(1);\n            }\n            input.value = value;\n        }\n\n        function viewTenant(id) {\n            showSnackbar('Detalle del condominio - Próximamente');\n        }\n\n        // Snackbar\n        function showSnackbar(message, type = 'info') {\n            const snackbar = document.getElementById('snackbar');\n            document.getElementById('snackbar-message').textContent = message;\n            \n            if (type === 'error') {\n                snackbar.style.background = '#ba1a1a';\n            } else if (type === 'success') {\n                snackbar.style.background = '#006c4c';\n            } else {\n                snackbar.style.background = 'var(--md-sys-color-on-surface)';\n            }\n            \n            snackbar.classList.add('active');\n            setTimeout(hideSnackbar, 5000);\n        }\n\n        function hideSnackbar() {\n            document.getElementById('snackbar').classList.remove('active');\n        }\n\n        function logout() {\n            localStorage.removeItem('token');\n            localStorage.removeItem('user');\n            window.location.href = '/';\n        }\n    </script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"
}

## ✅ Dashboard Refactorizado - Material Expressive

### Nuevo Diseño Material Design 3

| Elemento | Descripción |
|----------|-------------|
| **Navigation Rail** | Sidebar colapsable con hamburger menu (80px ↔ 280px) |
| **Material Icons** | Iconos `Material Symbols Rounded` |
| **FAB** | Floating Action Button para crear condominios |
| **Cards** | Elevaciones, bordes redondeados (12-16px), sombras sutiles |
| **Modal** | Diálogo estilizado con progress indicator y animaciones |
| **Text Fields** | Estilo Material con labels flotantes |
| **Snackbar** | Notificaciones tipo toast en la parte inferior |

### Características implementadas:

**🎨 Sistema de Diseño Material**
- Paleta de colores con `primary`, `secondary`, `surface` containers
- Variables CSS para fácil personalización
- Radio de bordes consistente (8-28px)
- Sombras elevación 1, 2, 3

**📱 Sidebar Responsive**
- Botón hamburger para colapsar/expandir
- Transiciones suaves (300ms)
- Labels que desaparecen en modo colapsado
- Icons siempre visibles

**🎯 Modal de Creación (3 pasos)**
1. **Paso 1**: Datos del condominio (nombre, slug, dirección)
2. **Paso 2**: Datos del admin (nombre, email, cédula, teléfono)
3. **Paso 3**: Review y confirmación

**✨ Animaciones**
- Slide-in para cards
- Ripple effects en botones
- Modal scale animation
- Number counter animation en stats

**🔔 Snackbar**
- Posición bottom-center
- Colores según tipo (info, success, error)
- Auto-dismiss a los 5 segundos

### Navegación por secciones:
- 📊 Dashboard (KPIs + Actividad)
- 🏢 Condominios (Tabla + Filtros + FAB)
- 👥 Usuarios (Próximamente)
- 📋 Auditoría (Logs globales)
- ⚙️ Configuración (Próximamente)

¿Te gusta el nuevo diseño? ¿Necesitas ajustes en algún elemento?
